### Page 4

Taiwan PCB & Laminates
29 October 2026 Citi Research
4
Upward earnings revisions; reiterate our Buy on both names — We raise EMC’s
2026/27 /28E earnings by 20%/31%/44% to factor in price hikes and enhanced
product mix. We thus raise EMC’s TP to NT$7,400 (28x 2027E EPS) from NT$6,000
(30x 2027E EPS). For TUC, we raise 2026/27 /28E earnings by 21%/29%/27% to
factor in price hike and enhanced product mix as well. We lift our TUC TP to
NT$2,300 (23x 2027E EPS) from NT$1,950 (25x 2027E EPS). We lower PE multiple

for both names given recent AI concerns amid the rate hike backdrop.

Taiwan PCB & Laminates

12
Elite Material
Company description
Established in 1992 in Taoyuan, Taiwan, EMC is a global leading copper clad
laminate (CCL) and prepreg supplier for HDI and smartphone applications.
EMC has developed several green materials products, from Mid Loss, Low
Loss, Very Low Loss, Ultra Low Loss down to Extreme Low Loss categories. In
the past few years, EMC has continued to gain presence in AI datacenter,

networking and HPC sectors. In the AI server market, EMC is one of the key
suppliers to global AI GPU customers and a major supplier to US CSPs for AI
ASIC projects.
Investment strategy
We are positive over EMC’s long-term growth outlook as we believe its
technology leadership in high-end CCL should position it well to benefit from
the structural spec migration in PCB materials amid the AI demand boom. We
expect EMC to maintain its market share in AI GPU projects and to gradually

gain share in next-gen ASIC projects in 2026. Given its leading position in
high-end CCL, sufficient raw material support, expanding capacity and solid
earnings outlook, we rate EMC a Buy.
Valuation
Our target price for EMC is set at NT$7,400, based on a target PE multiple of
28x on our 2027E EPS average. We believe our target PE multiple, at the peak
of its forward PE average in the past 5 years, is justified by a strong earnings
outlook supported by its leading position in CCL supply for AI GPU and ASIC

(high m/s) from 2026E onwards. We also believe the continued spec upgrade
for CCL, price hikes and product mix enhancement should help support its
margin expansion going forward. At our target price, the shares would trade at
55x/28x 2026E/27E PB.
Risks
Citi’s quant system rates EMC high-risk given high share price volatility, which
we largely attribute to frequent debates over the spec upgrade for next-gen AI
servers. In general, we believe some investors initially tend to have high hope

for aggressive spec upgrade without sufficiently considering the scalability
and cost structure of CCL products, which then leads to a shortfall in the end.
However, from a longer-term perspective, we see a decent and clear spec
upgrade trend with meaningful ASP growth potential among AI servers of
different generations. As such, we do not assign a high-risk rating.
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target
price include: 1) a slower-than-expected CCL upgrade trend; 2) weaker-than-

expected AI server demand; 3) production bottlenecks in AI supply chain (eg,
glass, copper foil, foundry or OSAT); 4) unexpected share loss in key AI server
projects; and 5) stricter practice of OOC policy by end customers.
TUC
Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 13

Taiwan PCB & Laminates
29 October 2026 Citi Research
13
Company description
TUC was established in 1974 and mainly produced optical glass initially. Since
1997, it started to provide copper clad laminate (CCL) and prepreg.
Headquartered in Hsinchu, Taiwan, TUC has an extensive global service
network spanning mainland China, Japan, Korea, the US and Germany. It has
been specializing in high-speed CCL for networking and now has a strong
position in supplying 800G switch opportunities. The company also started

to supply AI ASIC from 2025.
Investment strategy
We expect increasing AI ASIC exposure for TUC as it continues to see new
project opportunities among US CSP customers, including the high-end CCL
market for Trainium chip. We like TUC as it has production expertise in high-
speed CCL products, especially in networking, such as 800G, and has long
built solid relationships with upstream high-end materials suppliers to ensure
raw material support. We rate TUC a Buy given meaningful 800G ramp from

2026E onwards and its increasing opportunities in AI projects within CSPs
and enterprise players.
Valuation
Our target price for TUC is set at NT$2,300, based on a target PE multiple of
23x on our 2027E EPS. We believe our target PE multiple, at the peak of TUC’s
PE average in the last upcycle, is justified by its strong margin expansion
potential on a higher sales mix from AI ASIC server/800G products and price
hikes on supply tightness. We believe TUC’s growth outlook is well supported

by AI ASIC and 800G with its capacity ramp in Thailand from 3Q26 onward.
At our target price, the shares would trade at 2026E/27E PB of 53x/23x.
Risks
Citi’s quant system rates TUC High Risk given high share price volatility, which
we largely attribute to frequent debates over the spec upgrade for next-gen AI
servers. In general, we believe some investors initially tend to have high hope
for aggressive spec upgrade without sufficiently considering the scalability

and cost structure of CCL products, which then leads to a shortfall in the end.
However, from a longer-term perspective, we see a decent and clear spec
upgrade trend with meaningful ASP growth potential among AI servers of
different generations. As such, we do not assign a High Risk rating.
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target
price include: 1) a slower-than-expected CCL upgrade trend; 2) weaker-than-
expected AI server/800G demand; 3) production bottlenecks in AI supply

chain (e.g., glass, copper foil, foundry, or OSAT); 4) unexpected share loss in
key AI server projects; 5) flexible practice of OOC policy by end customers;
and 6) slow development of the PCB industry in Southeast Asia.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.
Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk

rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment
rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered

stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating
and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also

suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price
chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in

exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the
expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility

or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:
Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside

call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day

period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be
different from and does not affect a stock’s fundamental equity rating, which reflects a longer-term total absolute return

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

## Document 158: 261102_ms_cloud-semis

Source PDF: all report pdf/261102_ms_cloud-semis.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 7

### Page 1

M
Update
Greater China Technology Semiconductors  | Asia
Pacific
Cloud Semis: Growing Cloud
Capex into 2027
Hyperscalers’ spending remains strong so far, with further
expansion signaled for 2027 amid FCF turning negative. Positive
read for cloud semis (Aspeed and Montage).
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Daniel Yen, CFA
Equity Analyst
Daniel.Yen@morganstanley.com                                                +886 2 2730-2863
Charlie Chan
Equity Analyst

Morgan Stanley appreciates your support in
the 2027 Extel Asia Research Survey. Voting
opens 10 November 2026.
Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
2027 capex growth is highlighted: The Big Four hyperscalers' combined C3Q capex
was US$185bn, +90% Y/Y. CY2026 guidance stands at approximately US$170bn for
Microsoft, US$195–205bn for Google, US$135–145bn for Meta, and US$220bn for
Amazon. Google and Amazon maintained guidance; Meta raised its spending floor

US$5bn. More importantly, Microsoft expects spending to accelerate in January–
June 2027, Google anticipates a significant increase in 2027, and Amazon expects
meaningfully higher spending. Meta also plans to keep investing aggressively.
Supply constraints remain the issue: Microsoft reported C3Q26 capex of US
$45.2bn and expects C4Q spending >US$50bn, with some capacity deliveries
shifting into 1H27. Google invested US$51.4bn and turned to third-party computing

late in C3Q to address supply constraints, with usage rising in C4Q. Meta invested
US$34.9bn, including finance lease principal payments; Amazon’s cash capex
reached US$75.9bn. Inference already contributes well over half of AWS AI revenue.
Debating monetization: Microsoft generated US$18.6bn of quarterly FCF and
expects positive FCF in FY2027. Google, Meta, and Amazon reported negative
quarterly FCF of US$9.1bn, US$2.1bn, and US$33.5bn, respectively. Google also

flagged depreciation, data center operating costs, and third-party computing
expenses as near-term cloud margin headwinds, alongside TPU inventory cash
requirements. Management remains focused on attractive long-term returns.
Cloud semis: Constructive read-through. Sustained server investment supports
Aspeed's and Montage's demand outlook; delayed capacity could shift revenue
between quarters, but it's just a timing issue. Approximately 75% of Microsoft’s C3Q

capex targeted short-lived assets, including CPUs, GPUs, and higher memory/storage
costs, up from two-thirds mentioned before. Google allocated ~65% to servers and
networking.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
2027 capex is highlighted: Microsoft, Google and Amazon flag higher spending;
Meta remains committed to aggressive investment.
Capacity constraints, not budget: Microsoft’s delivery delays and Google’s third-
party computing usage point to infrastructure bottlenecks.
Cloud semis: Constructive read-through so far. Sustained hyperscaler spending

provides a favorable backdrop, with capacity additions raising demand.
November 2, 2026 01:07 AM GMT

Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 171: 261103_ms_PC-semis

### Page 1

M
Idea
Greater China  Semiconductors  | Asia Pacific
PC Semis: More divergent
outlooks
Business diversification, KGD (known good die) pricing power
and supply chain management create a divergent revenue
outlook for PC semis into 4Q and likely also 1Q27. Downgrade
Elan to EW.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Daniel Yen, CFA
Equity Analyst
Daniel.Yen@morganstanley.com                                                +886 2 2730-2863
Charlie Chan
Equity Analyst

Morgan Stanley appreciates your support in
the 2027 Extel Asia Research Survey. Voting
opens 10 November 2026.
Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
What’s Changed
Elan Microelectronics Corp
(2458.TW) From To
Rating Overweight Equal-weight
Price Target NT$250.00 NT$160.00
A much tougher 4Q… We mentioned in the prior PC semis note that the FOMP
(fear of missing procurement) could lead to  stable PC semis shipments in 3Q; now,

the inventory accumulation in 1Q-3Q26 could cause a deeper correction in 4Q.  PC
semis customers currently still aim for only a 5-10% Q/Q procurement decline in 4Q.
That said, the downside surprise in Parade’s 4Q outlook (link), which calls for a 20-
25% Q/Q revenue decline, could suggest a worse component correction. The still-
elevated Windows activation period of 200+ days and much smaller price hikes
from PC semis customers in 2H vs. 1H could suggest ongoing weakness into 1Q27.

But why are some companies resilient? Many investors are asking why Realtek’s
revenue could be up Q/Q in 4Q amid the PC correction (link). We cite three factors:
1) Consumer demand (other than PC/smartphones) is not necessarily all bad,
especially for IoT devices (low memory content). Espressif is guiding for  Q/Q
revenue growth in 4Q, following sequential growth in 3Q. 2) Price hikes reflect
increases at both  foundries/OSATs and in KGD. For example, TV SoC pricing is

several times higher than in past years due to  KGD price hikes (mainly DDR4). 3)
Large-scale IC design houses usually have better supply chain management and
lower exposure to PC.
No new growth driver into 2027: With the recent debate on cloud AI, we think it’s
still a matter of time before edge AI  takes off. However, we haven’t seen a clear
growth driver for PC semis. Preliminary outlooks from PC semis customers range
from flattish to down 20% Y/Y in 2027. Thus, it's  difficult to turn positive now.

Reduce earnings: We revise down Elan's PT and earnings and cut the stock to EW.
We appreciate the company's growth from smart Edge AI devices, but its exposure
to NB is still too significant to overlook. Our PT implies 16x our downward-revised
2027 EPS. Stay EW  Realtek and UW  Novatek, ASMedia, Parade, and Nuvoton.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
The FOMP (fear of missing procurement for PC semis in 1Q-3Q26) has caused a
deeper correction for some PC semis into 4Q.
It's easier for large-scale PC semis  to navigate through the downturn, while small
design houses are more concentrated on PC.
We stay conservative on PC semis, with no OW and await the cycle bottom

sometime in 2027.
November 3, 2026 09:00 PM GMT

2
Elan Micro: Earnings estimate revisions
We revise our EPS estimates by +1%/-20%/-16% for 2026/27/28: For 2026, we
incorporate actual 2Q26 results, which benefited from investment gains. For 2027-28, we
reduce our revenue and GM forecasts due to continued weak PC demand, which we
believe cannot be fully offset by the non PC business (mainly the drone market).
Exhibit 1: Elan: Estimate revisions
Source: Company data, Morgan Stanley Research (e) estimates.

Exhibit 2: Elan: Quarterly financial statement
Source: Company data, Morgan Stanley Research (e) estimates
Valuation Methodology
We cut our price target to NT$160: This reflects our earnings estimate revisions for
2026-28. Our price target is our base-case scenario value, which we continue to derive
from our residual income model. We reduce our intermediate growth rate estimate from
10.0% to 8.0% to reflect a weak PC business outlook. We reduce our terminal growth rate

estimate from 4.5% to 3.0% to better align it with broader economic growth. We continue
to assume a cost of equity of 9.6% and a payout ratio of 80%.
Our bull case value is cut to NT$234 from NT$366, and our bear case value is cut to NT
$84 from NT$131.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

欣銓 (3264 TT)  Buy

晶圓大廠新訂單逐步向上，建議 Buy。  
投資建議 ：欣銓 2026 年資本支出轉向積極擴張，主要來自與策
略客戶 (晶圓代工廠 )已確定之 ASIC 合作案，其龍潭新廠無塵室
與基礎設施建置，以及子公司全智科配合美系客戶擴產。 1Q26
至 3Q26 期間取得不動產、廠房及設備 (資本支出 )累計達 102.74
億元，相較於 3Q25 累計之 27.96 億元大幅增加 74.78 億元
(YoY+267.45%) 。3Q26 已進入量產，下一階段擴產將自 2Q27 啟
動，規劃自 2Q27 起每季開出一層全新無塵室加入量產，後續樓
層無塵室擴建將以現有主要客戶需求為優先配合對象。預估欣
銓 2027 年稅後 EPS 13.20 元。龍潭廠新專案是 2026~2027 年最
重要的增長引擎。而旗下全智湖口新廠，也於 3Q26 正式進入矽
光子相關新產品的量產階段，都將帶動公司中長期營收獲利向
上，因此建議欣銓 Buy ，目標價 322 元。  
3Q26 稅後 EPS 3.01 元：龍潭新廠歷經 1H26 客戶驗證與進場裝
機後，於 07/2026 起正式進入量產階段，帶動欣銓營收成長，
3Q26 營收為 53.37 億元， QoQ+17.72% 。營收依應用別分析，通
訊 Communication/Connectivity 佔 41.0% ；車用 / 安控
(ATV/Security) 佔 17.9% ；射頻晶片 (RF IC) 佔 10.9% ；微處理器
(General MCU) 佔 8.7%；Storage 佔 9.5%；PC/Consumers 佔 6.6%；
Memory 佔 3.7% ；其他 1.8% 。毛利率的部份， 3Q26 毛利率
45.43% ，增加 5.27 個百分點。 3Q26 稅後 EPS 3.01 元。  
預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 3.03 元：受到 3Q26 營收基期墊高、歐美
客戶於 11/2026 至 12/2026 進入假期拉貨淡季 ， 以及手機與通訊
終端產品季節性調節，營收僅小幅成長，毛利率展望方面，由
於 4Q26 未有新無塵室開出，且公司需提前為 2027 年擴產招募
人力、提列初期整備成本與設備折舊增加，預期 4Q26 毛利率將
略低於 3Q26 之 45.43% ，但仍可穩健維持高檔水準；公司努力
進行成本管控和改善產品組合 ， 讓 4Q26 獲利有機會高於 3Q26。
預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 3.03 元。 2026 年稅後 EPS 10.28 元。

2 群益投顧 
  欣銓 ESG ：  
欣銓積極推動  ESG( 環境、社會、公司治理 )相關政策與行動，並於官方網站
設有專屬  ESG 頁面與歷年永續報告書，公開揭露永續發展成果與目標。  
環境 (Environmental) 
•持續推動再生能源與減碳：延續加入  RE100 的承諾，積極推動節能減碳
與再生能源採購，並簽署 SBTi( 科學基礎減碳目標倡議 )承諾，強化減碳目標
與國際接軌。  
•循環經濟新提案：攜手供應鏈夥伴推動循環箱再進化，落實資源再利用與
減廢。  
•生物多樣性與生態尊重：持續舉辦淨灘、認養友善田等活動，強調尊重生
態、友善健康。  
社會 (Social) 
•同仁參與與文化深化：舉辦「 ESG Award 」專案競賽，鼓勵員工提出永續點
子與專案，涵蓋綠色製造、責任供應鏈、多元包容職場、人才培育、關懷弱
勢等五大方向。  
• 員工關懷與職場環境：持續改善員工宿舍環境，提升同仁生活品質。  
•社會公益：贊助在地傳統藝文活動、捐助弱勢團體，並與產業園區合作舉
辦移工籃球賽，促進多元共融。  
公司治理 (Governance) 
•資訊透明與永續報告：發行永續報告書，持續自主揭露非財務資訊，強調
資訊透明與溝通成效。  
•國際認證與標準：完成 RBA VAP 8.0 改版訓練，強化人權、環保等國際標
準合規與超越法規要求。  
•永續獎項肯定：榮獲「英國標準協會 (BSI)ESG 永續發展領航獎」及「台灣
企業永續獎 (TCSA) －永續報告獎金獎」 ，肯定公司在永續發展、資訊揭露與
社會責任的表現。  
攜手合作夥伴擴大永續影響力，推動以大帶小、標竿學習。展現穩健落實永
續目標與全球標準接軌的實力。  
◼CDP 氣候問卷榮獲  A-List 最高肯定 ： 公司在2025 年  CDP 全球環境評
比中，從全球逾  22,100 家企業脫穎而出，「氣候變遷」問卷榮獲 A 級領
導等級，「水安全」蟬聯  A- 領導等級肯定。  
◼ SBTi 近程目標通過驗證：公司設定的近程減碳目標已於 04/2026 正式通
過科學基礎減量目標倡議 (SBTi) 官方驗證。  
◼連續  9 年榮獲「公司治理評鑑前  5% 」的殊榮。  
◼龍潭廠榮獲「 2026 台灣建築永續獎金獎」肯定。  
欣銓 3Q26 營收 53.37 億元， QoQ+17.72% ，稅前淨利 18.25 億元，
QoQ+25.16% ，稅後 EPS 3.01 元：

### Page 3

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 10 月 28 日

3 群益投顧 
由於全球  AI 需求極其暢旺，許多一線封測同業將產能高度集中在  AI 核
心晶片，導致其他「 AI 周邊晶片」或「非  AI 通訊晶片」的測試產能出現
缺口。欣銓因此獲得大量的外溢訂單 (轉單 )，特別是在通訊與 PC Consumer 
領域。欣銓 3Q26 營收為 53.37 億元， QoQ+17.72% 。  
營收依應用別分析，通訊 Communication/Connectivity 佔 41.0% ；車用 /安控
(ATV/Security) 佔 17.9%； 射頻晶片(RF IC) 佔 10.9%； 微處理器(General MCU)
佔 8.7% ； Storage 佔 9.5% ； PC/Consumers 佔 6.6% ； Memory 佔 3.7% ；其他
1.8% 。  
3Q26 終端應用結構，通訊與連網 (Communication/Connectivity) 為最大成長
引擎，營收佔比自 2Q26 的 33.4% 大幅擴張至 41.0% 。這部份含光通訊和晶
圓代工廠的訂單 。 車用與安控(ATV/Security) 營收佔比為 17.9%，略 低 於2Q26
的 18.2% ，不過營收金額是增加，車用市場回溫，營收持續向上。射頻晶片
(RF IC) 營收佔比為 10.9% ，低於 2Q26 的 12.2% ，因為手機拉貨動能下滑。
儲存裝置 (Storage) 營收佔比自 2Q26 的 10.8% 收斂至 9.5% ，因晶圓代工廠的
產能受限。通用型微控制器 (General MCU) 佔比為 8.7% ，相較 2Q26 之 8.5%
微幅增加。個人電腦與消費性晶片 (PC/Consumers) 佔比為 6.6% ，相較 2Q26
之 9.9% 明顯下降，需求受到終端價格上漲影響，加上客戶產品組合上也會
優先供應通訊產品，營收佔比下滑較明顯。記憶體 (Memory) 營收佔比為
3.7% ，低於 2Q26 的 5.4% 。其他類別佔比則由 1.60% 微升至 1.80% 。  
毛利率的部份，得益於通訊連網晶片拉貨動能強勁以及整體稼動率維持高
檔約 80%。營業毛利達 24.25 億元， QoQ+33.1% ， YoY+89.1% ，毛利率攀升
至 45.43% ，較 2Q26 增加 5.27 個百分點，高稼動率帶動規模經濟發酵。營
業利益達 18.6 億元， QoQ+35.0% ，營益率達 34.8% ，較 2Q26 成長 4.4 個百
分點，較 3Q25 大幅增加 10.5 個百分點。業外收支部分， 3Q26 業外淨支出
為 0.34 億元，稅前淨利 18.25 億元， QoQ+25.16% ，稅後 EPS 3.01 元。創下
單季獲利新高紀錄。  
累計前三季 (1Q26 至 3Q26) 合併營收達 138.54 億元， YoY+35.4% ，毛利率達
41.7% ，營業利益達 43.34 億元， YoY+69.60% ，營益率達 31.3% ，累計稅後
淨利為 35.54 億元， YoY+78.4% ，前三季累計 EPS 達 7.49 元。  
就客戶類型分析 ，1Q26 至 3Q26 累計營收中 ， 無晶圓廠IC 設計業者 (Fabless)
貢獻 52.5% ，垂直整合製造商 (IDM) 佔 42.1% ，晶圓代工廠 (Foundry) 佔比由
前三季累計的 1.8% 提高至 5.4%，其他客戶佔比為 0.1%。在產品測試型態方
面，前三季晶圓測試 (Wafer Sorting) 營收佔比達 73.2% ，成品測試 (Final Test)
佔比為 26.1% ，其他項目佔 0.7% 。  
基於與策略客戶已談定的  ASIC 合作案，預估欣銓 2026 年資本支出將明
顯增加至約 130 億元， 2027 年將會高於 2026 年：  
欣銓對資本支出一向保持保守與謹慎，主因是高額折舊若遇上稼動率下滑，
會嚴重衝擊毛利率。此次  2026 年轉向積極擴張，主要來自與策略客戶已確
定的  ASIC 合作案。欣銓 2024 至 2025 年的資本支出主要集中在龍潭新廠
的廠房興建與土建尾款； 2026 年則轉向以「機台設備」為主，廠房支出比
例將顯著降低。先進測試機台交期長達 6 - 8 個月。目前測試機台約 1,700
台以上。  
欣銓在 1Q26 至 3Q26 期間取得不動產 、 廠房及設備(資本支出 )累計達 102.74

### Page 4

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 10 月 28 日

4 群益投顧 
億元，相較於 3Q25 累計之 27.96 億元大幅增加 74.78 億元， YoY+267.45% ，
資本支出顯著放量主要由兩大核心動能驅動：第一為龍潭新廠無塵室與基
礎設施建置，第二為子公司全智科技為配合美系通訊大客戶強勁 AI 晶片測
試需求而積極採購高階測試設備。 4Q26 因實際投入量產的機台數量相對有
限，單季資本支出將較 3Q26 持平或微幅縮減，預估 2026 年資本支出約 130
億元。但 2027 年資本支出規模掌握度高且明確將超越 2026 年。主因龍潭新
廠單一樓層無塵室與機台設備支出即達 40 億元以上，公司規劃自 2Q27 起
每季開出 1 層全新無塵室加入量產， 2027 年約有 3 個樓層陸續投產，且後
續樓層的無塵室擴建將以現有主要客戶需求為優先配合對象。  
截至 3Q26 底，公司不動產、廠房及設備帳面價值擴張至 308.94 億元，佔總
資產比重達 64% ，資產總計同步推升至 485.56 億元。  
為支應擴產資金需求，公司除於 1Q26 至 3Q26 透過銀行借款淨增 61.43 億
元外，子公司於 09/2026 處分欣銓股票取得約 40 億元流動資金，後續將以
股利形式匯回母公司以支應資本支出。為因應 2027 年更為龐大的資本支出
銓現階段仍以銀行借款為優先籌資工具，預期負債比率將自 3Q26 底的 50%
進一步拉升，但公司承諾將整體負債比率嚴格控制在 60% 以內，並視擴產階
段適時評估其他籌資工具；同時因擴建資金留存考量，現金股利配發率預計
將由 2026 年的 70% 常態區間下修至 2027 年的 50% 以上。  
欣銓在海外生產基地的布局策略上，維持專注鞏固台灣、新加坡及中國南京
等現有廠區的營運效率與產能發揮。管理層明確指出，赴新國家或地區設立
生產基地必須具備足夠的在地客戶基礎與充沛的訂單量支撐，才能避免新
廠陷入長期營運虧損；基於當前尚未取得足額訂單承諾的考量，欣銓目前並
無赴美國投資建廠的計畫。  
在現有海外據點的產品與客戶定位方面，高階 AI ASIC 與先進製程相關的
測試訂單全數集中於台灣本地廠區，包括龍潭廠及全智科湖口廠。新加坡廠
則以承接成熟產品線為主，產品結構涵蓋車用、工控及消費性晶片，主要原
因在於新加坡當地目前尚無大量先進晶圓代工產能；後續欣銓在新加坡廠
區的成長策略，將聚焦於進一步深化與聯電 (UMC) 及世界先進 (VIS) 在新加
坡當地晶圓廠的委外測試合作機會。  
折舊費用方面，欣銓已公告自 1/1/2025 起，部分機器設備耐用年限由 7 年
延長至 9 年，這將有助於反映資產實際經濟效益，欣銓 2025 年折舊費用
31.39 億元。龍潭廠量產機台之折舊費用已有部分於 3Q26 開始提列，但因
部分設備驗收時點落於季中之後，折舊並未全額反映在 3Q26 單季，預期
4Q26 折舊費用將進一步高於 3Q26 ，預估 2026 年折舊費用約 35 億元。進入
2027 年後，新設備折舊將隨各季機台進場與驗收進度按部就班提列；依產
業慣例，新機台通常需於投產前一季進場進行裝機與驗證，例如 2Q27 投產
之產能將於 1Q27 進場裝機，驗收完成後即開始提列折舊。儘管折舊費用持
續墊高且 4Q26 至 1Q27 提前招募人力與產能整備將使毛利率自 3Q26 的
45.43% 高點略為回落，但因龍潭廠 4Q26 產能利用率維持滿載、全智湖口新
廠產能將於 2027 年達全產全銷，產能高稼動率預期可有效稀釋固定折舊成
本，使單季毛利率持續穩健維持在 40% 以上。  
產能利用率已達滿載，預估欣銓 4Q26 營收 55.24 億元， QoQ+3.50% ，稅前
淨利 18.57 億元， QoQ+1.75% ，稅後 EPS 3.03 元：  
展望 4Q26 營運，欣銓預估合併營收將呈現 QoQ+3% 左右，主要受到 3Q26

5 群益投顧 
營收基期墊高、歐美客戶於 11/2026 至 12/2026 進入假期拉貨淡季，以及手
機與通訊終端產品季節性調節所致。  
旗下全智總坪數逾 3,500 坪的湖口新廠，也於 3Q26 正式進入矽光子相關新
產品的量產階段，預期 4Q26 可以開出 20-25% 的產能，全智湖口廠客戶需
求高，因此 2027 年可以達到滿載狀況。  
毛利率展望方面，由於 4Q26 未有新無塵室開出，且公司需提前為 2027 年
擴產招募人力、提列初期整備成本與設備折舊增加，預期 4Q26 毛利率將略
低於 3Q26 之 45.43% ，但仍可穩健維持在高檔水準。定價策略方面，欣銓對
主力客戶價格維持穩定，但已針對低毛利與零星客戶提出調價，部分客戶已
順利反映漲價。公司努力進行成本管控和改善產品組合，讓 4Q26 獲利有機
會高於 3Q26。預估欣銓 4Q26 營收 55.24 億元， QoQ+3.50% ，稅前淨利 18.57
億元， QoQ+1.75% ，稅後 EPS 3.03 元。  
預估欣銓 2026 年營收 193.78 億元， YoY+38.13% ，稅前淨利 63.05 億元，
YoY+74.58% ，稅後 EPS 10.28 元。  
欣銓提供完整的光電測試 (全智 )與先進異質整合封裝 (瑞峰 )解決方案，建構
完整 CPO 生態系：  
欣銓集團在矽光子領域的布局完整，透過結合母公司與旗下轉投資公司的
資源，建構完整 CPO( 共封裝光學 )生態系，欣銓與全智皆為  SEMI 矽光子
產業聯盟及異質整合矽光子產業聯盟的重要成員。欣銓已布局光通訊與矽
光子 (CPO) 高階測試架構，目前接洽並配合之客戶達三家以上，測試製程至
少已切入至 Insertion 1 和 3 階段，並持續往 Insertion 4 進行技術評估與了
解。由於目前全球矽光子晶片出貨量仍處於技術開發與初期工程驗證階段，
市場尚未進入大規模營收爆發期，因此公司尚未設定具體之 CPO 營收貢獻
目標，但已透過全智湖口廠與美系通訊核心客戶建立緊密夥伴關係，提早卡
位高速傳輸光電整合封裝之長期測試商機。  
欣銓集團的矽光子佈局中，全智科技 (欣銓 100% 轉投資 )主要負責  EIC 測
試、PIC 測試以及光電整合測試 。 其中 EIC 測試已進入量產多年 ， 並於 2024 
年建置矽光子專用的光電整合測試實驗室，提供全方位的工程測試與驗證
服務。瑞峰半導體 (欣銓投資約  25%) 專注於先進異質整合封裝 (如 CPO 3D 
TSV/CoW ， EIC+PIC) 。瑞峰負責將 EIC 晶圓疊加在 PIC 晶圓上 (Chip on 
Wafer) ，隨後再交由全智進行光電整合測試。 Fabrinet( 策略投資夥伴 )主要負
責後續的  CPO 相關製程與光學引擎等項目。  
全智 (湖口新廠 )3Q26 正式量產，總面積超過  3,500 坪的湖口新廠，將著重
於矽光子測試。根據客戶端訊息，該廠區矽光子新產品的產能利用率拉升速
度預期將會相當快，且已與客戶洽談，有機會在  2027 年接近滿載。在  CPO 
的  EIC/PIC 測試產能上，目前已佈建超過  150 台設備。中長期擴廠計畫，
為因應強勁的光通訊客戶需求，湖口廠目前正在整理無塵室空間，希望能再
騰出 1~2 層樓來配合擴充；同時，為 2028 年以後的長期成長，公司也已準
備著手尋求購買新土地。  
瑞峰已於  2024 年建置第一條  12 吋矽光子晶圓級封裝 (WLP) 生產線。目
前將  EIC 疊在  PIC 晶圓上的  Chip on Wafer 製程正逐漸進入量產。 2025 
年已導入數家新客戶，進行超過  30 項  NPI( 新產品導入 )驗證； 2026 年更

6 群益投顧 
預計會有超過  10 家新客戶 (Tape-out / New design) 進行導入與設計中。  
預估欣銓 2027 年營收 251.13 億元， YoY+29.60% ，稅前淨利 81.39 億元，
YoY+29.08% ，稅後 EPS 13.20 元：  
展望 2027 年營運前景，欣銓管理層對整體營收增長維持正面樂觀基調，主
要受惠於 2 大核心擴產引擎全面啟動：第一為龍潭新廠無塵室自 2Q27 起進
入逐季開出階段，第二為子公司全智科技新竹湖口新廠產能將有機會達到
全產全銷。  
除晶圓代工廠外包訂單外，美系通訊核心客戶對高階 AI 晶片測試需求自
4Q26 一路強勁延伸至 2027 全年，全智湖口新廠產能已獲客戶全數包下，若
產能利用率達到滿載運作下，預期有機會貢獻 2027 年單月營收 1~1.5 億元。
車用與安控 (ATV/Security) 及儲存裝置 (Storage) 受惠於 Edge AI 與資料中心
需求回溫， 2027 年營收貢獻將持續擴張。  
季度走勢上，延續 4Q26 營收 QoQ+1~3% 的走勢， 1Q27 受到農曆春節工作
天數減少與季節性影響，預估營收將持平於 4Q26 ，毛利率受前期準備費用
影響維持在 40% 以上高檔水準，整體產能利用率 (UTR) 則維持約 80% 健康水
位。真正的成長將自 2Q27 啟動，欣銓龍潭廠共有 7 層無塵室，第 1 層已於
2Q26 年完成認證，自 07/2026 正式開始貢獻營收與量產。第 2 至第 3 層無
塵室工程預計於 2026 年底至 1H27 完工，其中第 2 層產能最快自 2Q27 開始
貢獻營運，依每季啟用一層的節奏逐步推進。  
獲利能力與定價方面，雖然新廠開出初期伴隨折舊與人力整備等成本，但隨
著 AI 測試需求暢旺帶動整體產能利用率持續站穩高檔，加上高階晶圓測試
比重維持高檔，評估 2027 年毛利率將高於 2026 年。此外，公司針對主要長
期配合之大型客戶維持穩定報價，但對於低毛利與零星客戶已啟動調價提
議並陸續反映漲價，進一步強化整體產品組合獲利體質。  
預估欣銓 2027 年營收 251.13 億元， YoY+29.60% ，稅前淨利 81.39 億元，
YoY+29.08% ，稅後 EPS 13.20 元。

10 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

3Q26 Review: EV 판매 증가의 역풍 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 160,000원(유지) 
 현재주가 95,600원(10/30)

 동사의 3분기 실적은 매출액 31.6조원(+10.1%yoy), 영업이익 1.8조원
(+24.3%yoy, OPM 5.8%), 지배이익 1.29조원(-9.8%yoy)을 기록, 도매 판
매 증가로 인한 매출 증가에도 불구하고 인센티브/원자재 비용 증가로 인해 
컨센서스를 하회하는 실적 쇼크를 기록 
 영업이익 증감 요인: 인센티브&가격 -7,270억원, 믹스 -4,650억원, 재료비 
-3,680억원으로 총 1.56조원 비용 발생. 증가 요인은 판매량 +4,420억원, 
환율 +5,290억원, 기타비용 절감 +4,130억원으로 1.38조원 상쇄. 인센티
브로 인한 비용 증가 예상외로 컸는데 EV 판매량 증가 영향. 기말환율 하락
으로 판매보증충당금은 전년비 9,254억원 감소 
 전동화 믹스 증가: 글로벌 xEV 소매 판매 30만대(+47%yoy), 판매비중 
36.2%(+9.8%p yoy). HEV 17.2만대(+46%yoy), 텔루라이드, 스포티지 
HEV 공급 확대로 3분기 미국 HEV 판매 +118%yoy 기록. EV 12.3만대
(+75%yoy), 서유럽 EV 판매는 +163%yoy 기록 
 4분기 전망: 중동 분쟁 지속으로 원가 부담의 급격한 개선은 어려울 전망. 
연초 CID에서 제시한 연간 판매 목표는 달성 가능할 것으로 보이지만 기존 
수익성 목표를 하회할 전망. 중장기적으로 상품 경쟁력 강화, 지역별 파워트
레인 대응 전략 유지, 내부 원가 및 체질 개선으로 외부 비용 부담 상쇄를 
추진할 계획. 중동 분쟁 완화에 따른 비용 부담 해소와 HEV 비중 확대를 통
한 수익성 개선은 2027년 시점이 될 것으로 예상 
 단기 방향 전환 어려울 듯: 판매 호조에도 불구하고 저가형 EV 판매 비중 
증가함에 따라 수익성이 악화되는 모습이 3분기에도 재확인. 글로벌 수요 
변화를 감안했을 때, EV 시장 선점이 매우 중요한 것은 사실이지만 단기 수
익성 부담 커지고 있는 점 우려됨. 중국산 EV의 글로벌 확산, 중동 전쟁 장
기화에 따른 원가 부담 증대가 연말까지는 영향을 미칠 것. 동사에 대한 투
자의견 매수와 목표주가 16만원 유지함

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

투자기간 및 투자등급/투자의견 비율

## Document 252: [034020] 두산에너빌리티(034020) 시장과 산업의 시차. 2026 SMR 첫걸음. 2H27 원전 수 주 확대 본격화두산에너빌리티(034020) 시장과 산업의 시차_47af979a

Source PDF: all report pdf/[034020] 두산에너빌리티(034020) 시장과 산업의 시차. 2026 SMR 첫걸음. 2H27 원전 수 주 확대 본격화두산에너빌리티(034020) 시장과 산업의 시차_47af979a.pdf

유틸리티업종

◼ 목표주가 85,000원(하향). 3분기 실적, 소규모 SMR 수주 등은 긍정적 
◼ 구체적인 미국 원전 사업개발 시작, 2H27 장납기품목 선발주 기대 
◼ 2027년 체코 원전 시공 입찰 재시도, 시공능력의 중요성 강조

목표주가 85,000원(하향). 3분기 실적 양호, 소규모 SMR 수주는 긍정적 
투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 85,000원으로 하향. 이제는 성장 스토리보다
는 실질적인 원전 수주 확대가 필요한 상황. 의미 있는 수준의 원전 수주 확
대는 2H27부터 본격화될 가능성 존재. 다만, 단기적인 원전 수주 모멘텀 부
족 등을 감안해 6개월 목표주가를 현실화했음 
1) 3Q26 연결기준 영업이익 2,612억원(+91%, 이하 YoY), 에너빌리티 부문 
영업이익 1,211억원(+179%) 등 양호한 실적 기록. 이는 에너빌리티의 가스
터빈, 원전 기자재 매출 증가 등에 기인, 2) 10월 29일 NuScale과 1개 모듈 
주기기 공급 계약을 체결했으며, 4Q26 X-energy와 4개 모듈의 일부 장납기 
품목/소재 수주가 예상되는 등 2Q26 ~ 3Q26 TerraPower와의 일부 품목 계
약에 이어 개별 건의 규모는 작지만 다수의 SMR 관련 기자재 수주가 늘어나
는 점은 긍정적. 3) 최근 폴란드 AP1000 3기의 선발주 장납기 품목 확정(폴
란드 공급업체의 대상 생산 품목 확정, 원자로 압력용기 및 증기발생기는 미
확정)되었으며, 미국 원전 프로젝트가 구체화되기 시작. 4) 향후 AP1000향 
공급 기자재 품목 확대 가능성도 존재. 이는 2H27 본격적인 원전 수주금액 
확대로 이어질 것으로 기대

미국 원전은 구체적 사업개발 시작, 2H27 이후 장납기품목 선발주 기대 
2026년 6월 DOE의 ‘원전 공급망 금융지원 사업 발표(AP1000 10기)’, 8월 
TVA의 ‘2026 IRP’에 2040년까지 최대 원전 5.4GW 승인, 9월 한국과 미국 
상무부 간의 원전 투자 8기 MOU 체결 등을 통해 미국 원전 건설 재개 정책 
및 제도 개편이 구체화되고 강화되었음. 미국 원전 시장은 4Q26 ~ 2027년 
구체적인 사업개발 단계를 거쳐(부지 선정, 원전 운영 SPC 설립, 금융계약체
결, COL 신청 등), 2H27 ~ 2028년 장납기품목 선발주, 2028 ~ 2029년 건
설 계약 단계 진입 예상. 동사는 4Q26 미국 및 국내 가스터빈, 국내 공공 해
상풍력 프로젝트용 터빈, 폴란드 AP1000 주기기 등 미국 원전 이외 분야에
서의 수주 확대 예상. 현재 Rolls-Royce 등과도 SMR 주기기 공급 관련 논의 
중이며, NuScale SMR은 2027년 PPA 체결 시, COL 및 FID 이전 SMR 모듈 
11기(4Q26 1기 계약)의 수주가 가능할 것으로 기대

2027년 체코 원전 시공 입찰 재시도, 시공능력의 중요성 강조 
대우건설·두산에너빌리티 컨소시엄의 체코 원전 시공 계약 협상은 종료되었
지만, 2027년 7월 신규 시공사와 계약 예정. 동사는 입찰에 다시 참여할 예
정. 이번 이슈의 핵심 쟁점은 이미 체코 측과 공사비총액(Lump-sum) 계약을 
맺은 한수원이 시공사와 총액계약을 맺기를 원한 것임. 이번 이슈로 체코 두
코바니 원전 EPC 프로젝트 수익성에 대한 우려가 발생할 수 있지만, 실제 건
설 과정에서 추가 비용이 공사비 계약금액(예비비 포함)을 초과할지는 예단하
기 어려움. 하도급 관리, 공기 준수 등 시공능력의 중요성이 강조되는 상황. 
동사는 3년 전부터 체코에 시공 인력 상주하며 시공 환경 자료 확보, 초기 인
허가, 설계 업무 관여해 왔으며, 주기기 공급사로서 기자재 특성을 활용해 설
치까지 수행 가능한 경쟁력 등을 바탕으로 성공적 수주를 목표하고 있음

표 2. 웨스팅하우스의 미국 및 글로벌 신규 원전 프로젝트 현황 (초기 사업발굴 단계 제외)  
 프로젝트·사업 기수 발전용량 Cameco 분류 단계 및 비고 
미국 DOE 공급망 금융지원 사업 최대 10기 최대 11.5GW 장납기 품목 조달 금융지원, 7개 유틸리티와 논의 중 
 미 상무부 전략적 협력사업 최대 10기 최대 11.5GW 정부 협력사업(한국은 6기 투자/건설) 
 V.C. Summer 2·3호기 공사 재개 2기 2.3GW 장납기 품목 발주·EPC(LLI/EPC) 
폴란드 Lubiatowo-Kopalino 3기 3.5GW 장납기 품목 발주·EPC(LLI/EPC) 
불가리아 Kozloduy 7·8호기 2기 2.3GW 초기 서비스 계약(ESC) 
우크라이나 Khmelnytskyi 5·6호기 2기 2.3GW 초기 서비스 계약(ESC) 
정부 지원·협력 및 계약 기반 사업 소계  최대 29기 최대 33.4GW FEED·초기 사업발굴 제외 
FEED 사업 네덜란드, 슬로베니아, 핀란드, 스웨덴, 미국 합계 11기 합계 12.7GW 기본설계(FEED)

표 3. DOE의 미국 대형원전 공급망 금융지원 사업 
항목 공식 발표 내용 
조건부 대출 약정 규모 최대 175억 달러·US$17.5 billion 
지원 대상 최대 5개 프로젝트, 프로젝트당 AP1000 2기, 합계 10기 
프로젝트 소유 구조 Westinghouse와 해당 전력회사 또는 에너지기업이 공동 소유 
대출 접근 전 자기자본 요건 프로젝트당 Westinghouse 5억 달러, 상대 전력회사·에너지기업 5억 달러, 합계 10억 달러를 사전에 확약 
조달 방식 Westinghouse가 참여 기업과 함께 장납기품목을 고정가격으로 조달 
조달 시점 자기자본 확약 시점과 기타 조건에 따라 프로젝트별 구매를 순차적으로 진행 
자료: DOE, 대신증권 Research Center

5  
표 4. DOE의 미국 대형원전 공급망 금융지원 사업의 대상 장납기품목(LLI; Long-Lead Items) 
 품목 설명  대출과 관련된 확인 근거 
원자로압력용기 
(RPV; Reactor Pressure Vessel) 
핵연료와 노심을 수용하고 원자로 냉각재의 압력을 유지하는 
용기. 앞서 설명한 격납용기와 구분되는 원자로 주기기 DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23 
증기발생기 
(SG; Steam Generator) 
원자로에서 나온 열을 전달받아 터빈을 돌릴 증기를 만드는 대
형 열교환기 DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23 
사전제작 구조 모듈 
(PSM; Prefabricated Structural Modules) 
원전 건물의 벽체·바닥 등 구조물을 모듈로 미리 제작하는 방
식의 구성품. AP1000에는 강판과 콘크리트로 구성하는 구조 
모듈이 사용됨 
DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23. 다만, 미국 대
출자료에 개별 모듈 코드까지 열거되지는 않음 
원자로냉각재펌프 
(RCP; Reactor Coolant Pump) 
원자로 냉각재를 순환시켜 원자로에서 발생한 열을 증기발생
기로 전달하는 펌프 DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23 
격납용기 
(CV; Containment Vessel) 
원자로압력용기와 주요 설비를 둘러싸며 방사성 물질의 외부 
방출을 억제하는 대형 강철 밀폐 구조물 
DOE EDF 담당자의 직접 발언에서 확인 — 
2026.10.20 공개 인터뷰 
자료: DOE, 대신증권 Research Center

## Document 273: [373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 일회성 보상금 포함된 실적, ‘30년 ESS 수요 대비 배 이상 높은 셀 3사...LG에너지솔루션(373220) 일회성 보상금_7b277d22

Source PDF: all report pdf/[373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 일회성 보상금 포함된 실적, ‘30년 ESS 수요 대비 배 이상 높은 셀 3사...LG에너지솔루션(373220) 일회성 보상금_7b277d22.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-11-04
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

LG에너지솔루션 (373220)

Earnings Review Company Analysis | 이차전지/화학 | 2026. 11. 4  
일회성 보상금 포함된 실적, ‘30년 ESS 수요 대비 배 이상 높은 셀 3사 Capa 우려

AMPC 4,169억원 포함 영업이익 컨센서스 대폭 상회 
동사는 8일 매출액 9조 6,434억원(YoY 59%, QoQ 27%), AMPC 포함한 영업이익 
7,560억원(OPM 7.8%, YoY 26%, QoQ 567%)로 시장 Consensus(공정공시한 8일 
기준 3,264억원)을 대폭 상회하는 어닝 서프라이즈 발표. 회사는 ESS 출하 급증과 고
전압 Mid-Ni EV 파우치 확대, 견조한 EV향 2170-46시리즈 공급을 물량 중심을 성
장하였고, 폴란드 가동률 개선, 수익성이 견조한 원통형 물량 증가, 북미 ESS 생산성 
개선에 따른 고정비 부담 완화 및 고객사의 일회성 물량 보상금 영향으로 설명. 그러나 
일회성 물량 보상금의 구체적 금액, 반복 발생 여부는 제시하지 않아 불확실성 남겨. 
7,560억원의 영업이익에도 불구하고 영업외손실 9,872억원 발생으로 당기순손실 기
록. 이에는 소형 IT파우치 자산손상 반영되어, 가동률 개선 및 감가상각비 감소를 위해 
Capa를 조정하는 배터리 회사들의 대응 전략이 이번 실적에서 다시 확인

‘30년 ESS 수요 전망들 대비 국내사 Capa 배 이상 높아 
동사는 북미 ESS Capa 확대를 통한 탈중국 수요에 적극 대응하겠다는 전략. 파우치 
라인 추가 전환 및 신규 각형 라인 확보를 통해 북미 ESS Capa를 ‘26년말 50+에서 
‘27년 70+로 제시. 최근 타사에서 ‘26년 미국 ESS 수요를 80~100GWh, 혹은 
130+GWh로, ‘30년 두배 이상, 혹은 240+GWh로 수요 전망에 대해 답변한 바 있음. 
동사 전체 Capa 300GWh(‘25년 연말), SK On 179GWh, SDI 140GWh)임을 감안하
면 ‘30년 약 2배 수요를 가정하면 셀 3사 Capa의 32%에 달하는 수요, 만일 3배 수
요 가정하면 48.5% 수준. 북미 외 시장 가성비 중시 기조 및 중국 LFP 장악(‘25년 
97% M/S)으로 진입장벽 높음을 감안하면 미국 수요 중요. 이를 상쇄하기 위해 EV향 
배터리의 안정적인 공급 지속 필요하며, 이를 위해 동사 원통형은 TESLA의 비미국 시
장 판매량이, 파우치는 시장 자체 축소 지속 여부에 유의할 필요 
상대적으로 가장 견조한 출하량, 그러나 고평가 영역  
셀3사중 EV향 배터리 출하량 및 ESS 탑재에서 현재 가장 견조한 판매량을 보이고 있
으나, AMPC와 일회성 보상금이 영업손익의 상당부분을 견인한다는 점에서 수익성 취
약하다는 단점. 또한 ROE가 (-) 영역 임에도 PBR이 3.x내외로 거래되고 있어 고평가 
지적에서 자유롭기 어려워. 당분간 모회사인 LG화학의 PRS 발행에 따른 단기 오버행 
이슈도 관건. PBR 2.3 x(‘25년대비 하락하는 ROE 감안, 20% 할인)적용 목표주가 
290,000원 제시, HOLD 유지. 주식 투자 매력 낮은 상황으로 판단 
 Analyst 정경희 
khchung@ls-sec.co.kr 
Hold (유지)

목표주가 (하향) 290,000 원 
현재주가 338,000 원 
상승여력 -12.8 %

컨센서스 대비

EV향 3분기 data  
이차전지에서 EV향 비중은 여전히 가장 높다. 타입별로 보면 3분기 글로벌 EV향 이차전
지에서 각형의 M/S가 83%로 지속 증가하고 있으며, 동사가 생산하는 원통형과 파우치의 
경우 합산 M/S가 17%로 하락하였다. 현재 Wh 기준 동사 EV향 배터리에서 3분기 파우
치가 55%, 원통형이 45%로 파우치 비중이 10% 원통형을 상회한다.

나타난다.

그림 9 셀 3 사 분기별 EV 향 탑재량 글로벌 M/S 추이: M/S 감소중

자료: SNE 리서치, LS증권 리서치센터

밸류에이션, 수익성 대비 고평가 영역  
동사는 ‘24년 ROE -4.9%, ‘25년 -5.2%에 이어 ‘26년 -4.4%, ‘27년 -3.4%로 (-) 영역이 
지속되는 것으로 예상한다. ‘24년과 ‘25년 순이익은 (+)지만, 해외 합작회사가 많고 이들 이
익을 연결함에 따라 지배주주순이익은 적자를 기록한 것을 보인다. JV 규모가 축소되고 있
어 이러한 순이익과 지배주주 순이익상의 괴리는 좁혀지겠지만, ROE가 (-)영역 지속을 예
상함에 따라 현재 거래 PBR은 고평가 상황으로 볼 수 있다. 물론 이것은 향후 수익 성장
에 대한 시장 기대가 반영된 것으로 볼 수 있지만, 이차전지 6개사 평균 Consensus 대비 
실제 수익이 ‘23년부터 지속 하향되고 있다는 점, ESS대비 여전히 판매비중이 높은 EV에서 
출하량 정체가 일어나고 있다는 점, 미국 ESS에서 여전히 높은 중국 M/S를 감안하면 이러
한 기대 수준에 대해 다시 검토할 필요가 있다. 이에 더해, 모회사 기준에서는 필요한 과정
이지만 동사 주가 측면에서는 LG화학의 PRS 발행도 단기 오버행으로 당분간 주가에 부담
을 줄 수 있다.

그림 10 셀 3 사, 양∙음극재 3 사 추정치 변화율: ‘23 년부터 추정치 지속 하향 조정

자료: DataGuide, LS증권 리서치센터

미국 ESS가 모든 것을 해결할 수 있나? 
‘25년 글로벌 ESS 시장의 97%를 중국배터리사가 공급했다. LFP 각형이 표준화된 시장인 
것이다. 미국 시장만 중국에 대한 관세 허들, 정책 허들로 한국 배터리사에 기회로 작용한
다. 그러나 ‘26년부터 시작된 FEOC와 관세추가 부담에도 불구, 상반기 미국 ESS 시장의 
76%을 (기타사의 국적을 알 수 없기에 제외하더라도) 중국 셀사가 차지했다. 만일 한국 배
터리 유관회사들이 전망하듯 ‘30년 미국 ESS 시장이 160~200GWh 이상, 240+GWh, 혹
은 240~300GWh가 된다고 해도 3사 전체 Capa의 50%가 채 되지 못한다. EV 수요가 뒷
받침되지 않는다면 미국 ESS만으로는 가동률을 80~90% 이상 올리기 어렵다는 의미다. 특
히 동사는 원통형과 파우치를 생산하고 있으나, 원통형은 북미외 지역 TESLA의 판매에, 
파우치는 올해 출하량이 감소하는 것으로 나타나고 있다. 파우치 고객의 각형 채택 비중 
증가를 감안하면 동사 EV향 배터리 출하량 증가를 확신하기는 어렵다.   
긍정적 기대와 동시에 냉정한 점검이 필요한 시점으로 판단한다.

LS Securities Research 6 
표2 LG 에너지솔루션 3Q26 실적발표 주요 Q&A 
[Q&A]           
Q1-1. 4Q26 실적 전망과 2027년 큰 방향은? 
  A. 4Q26 ESS 출하량은 북미 수요 대응으로 QoQ 약 40% 증가 예상 
   유럽 고전압 Mid-Ni 파우치 및 원통형 물량도 연말까지 견조한 출하 지속 전망 
   3Q26 가동을 시작한 북미 JV 효과가 더해져 전사 매출은 QoQ 약 10% 성장 가능할 것으로 예상 
   가동률 개선과 사이트 안정적 램프업은 지속 추진하나 3Q26 일회성 요인 소멸의 기저효과 존재 
   4Q26 혼다 JV ESS CAPA 추가 가동 준비에 따른 초기 고정비 부담으로 수익성 일부 둔화 불가피

2027년 사업계획은 수립 중이며 전방 수요 변수로 구체적 가이던스 제시는 어려운 단계 
    ESS: 북미 확보 수주 기반으로 유의미한 물량 성장 지속 기대 
    소형: 전략 고객향 EV 원통형과 46 시리즈 신규 애플리케이션 판매로 안정적 성장 예상 
    EV 파우치: 중저가 제품 물량과 북미 EV 향 생산품이 더해져 성장 예상 
   전 사업부 매출 성장 기대하나 핵심 제품 경쟁력·기존 CAPA 효율화·가동률 극대화·원가 개선에 집중 
   2027년 매출 성장률·영업이익률 등 구체적 수치는 제시 X

Q1-2. 북미 PFE 요건 충족을 위한 LFP 공급망 전략과 중국 관련 주력 양극재 공급사 대응은? 
  A. 미국 생산세액공제 확보를 위해 직접재료비 내 PFE 소재 비중 요건을 충족하는 공급망 구축 필요 
   회사 설명상 PFE 소재 허용 비중은 2026년 40%에서 2027년 35% 이하로 강화. Non-PFE 비중과 혼동 유의 
   소재별 소싱 전략을 구체화해 준비 중이며 요건 충족은 차질 없이 진행될 것으로 판단 
   재료비 비중이 큰 양극재는 최근 계약한 국내 업체 외 복수 Non-PFE 업체 조달 옵션 확보

음극재·분리막·전해액도 기존 Non-PFE 공급망에 더해 신규 업체 적극 발굴 
   기존 원가 중심 최적화에서 정책·지정학 리스크를 고려한 공급망 안정성 강화로 전략 확대 
   협력사의 원가·기술·물량 대응력을 종합 평가하고 장기적으로 현지 협력사 발굴·현지화 추진 
   질문한 중국 관련 주력 공급사의 개별 지위·조달 비중에 대한 직접적인 상세 답변 X

Q2-1. ESS 사업의 수익성 전망 및 개선 방안은? 
  A. EV→ESS 라인 전환으로 수요에 맞는 생산체계를 구축하고 북미 고객 적기 공급에 주력 
   다수 사이트 동시 램프업에 어려움이 있었고 고정비 절감 노력에도 수익성 개선 속도는 시장 기대보다 느렸음 
   2026년 말 50GWh 목표 셀 라인 램프업은 마무리 국면. Link CAPA도 3Q26 에 이어 4Q26 추가 보강 예정 
   CAPA 안정화와 점진적인 고정비 부담 완화 기대 
   확보 CAPA 효율화, 소재 소싱 다변화로 원재료비·물류비 절감에 집중 
   AI 데이터센터 및 SI 프로젝트 비중 확대를 통한 고객 포트폴리오 최적화 추진 
   셀·시스템 고용량화로 원가 경쟁력을 높여 구조적 수익성 개선 추진 
   구체적 ESS 영업이익률 목표나 개선 폭은 제시 X

Q2-2. 북미 ESS 추가 수주 현황과 향후 모멘텀은? 
  A. 빠른 시장 성장 및 Non-PFE 에 우호적인 정책으로 북미 현지 생산 셀 수요 급증 
   현재 계획된 CAPA 기준으로 2028년까지 물량은 대부분 확정. 그 이후 물량 중심으로 고객 수주 협의 진행 
   기존 전력망·유틸리티 고객, 이들을 통한 AI 데이터센터 BESS 수요, 하이퍼스케일러 BTM 직접 구매 수요 포함 
   예상 수요가 공급 가능 CAPA를 초과해 전략적으로 중요한 프로젝트 중심으로 미래 잔여 CAPA 운영 계획 
   추가 고객명·신규 계약량 등은 제시하지 않음. 예상 수요와 확정 수주를 구분

Q3-1. 46시리즈의 구체적인 수주 현황과 향후 모멘텀은? 
  A. 기존 OEM 부터 EV 스타트업까지 이미 440GWh 이상 물량 확보 
   추가로 다수 글로벌 OEM 과 협의 중이며 가까운 시일 내 의미 있는 규모의 수주 성과 기대 
   제품 경쟁력·지역별 생산 역량으로 46 시리즈 선도 입지를 강화하고 고객 포트폴리오 확대 계획 
   440GWh는 확보 물량이며 연간 판매량이나 생산 CAPA가 아님. 추가 계약 규모·고객명은 미공개

LS Securities Research 7

Q3-2. Tabless 2170의 경쟁력과 수주·신규 시장 대응 현황은? 
  A. 기존 제품 대비 낮은 발열·저항으로 안전성 및 고출력에 유리해 HEV·BBU·로봇 등 다양한 용도에 적합 
   오창에서 전동공구용 제품은 이미 양산 중  
   BBU 향 LFP 2170은 2Q27 양산 목표로 준비 중 
   HEV 향은 2027년 말 애리조나에서 양산 계획  
   LFP 2170은 NCM 대비 열안전성 테스트에서 우수한 성능을 보여 고객의 긍정적 피드백 확보 
   고객 니즈·수요에 적극 대응해 신시장 경쟁력 확보 추진. 수주 규모 등 정량 정보는 제시하지 않음

Q4-1. 재무 건전성 개선 방안과 2027 년 자금 조달 계획은? 
  A. 선제 CAPA 투자 자금은 저리 정책자금 장기차입 및 원화·외화 회사채로 안정적으로 조달해 왔음 
   주요 투자는 대부분 집행됐고 CAPEX는 2025년을 기점으로 유의미하게 감소 중 
   향후 영업현금흐름 확대분을 차입금 상환에 우선 활용, 자산 유동화를 통한 추가 재원 확보도 병행 
   차입금·금융비용 부담을 축소해 재무지표 개선 및 건전성 확보 추진 
   2026년 본사 추가 차입 계획은 없으며 해외법인은 기존 약정 차입금 일부 추가 인출 예정 
   2027년은 기존 차입금 차환·운전자금 범위에서 최소한의 자금 조달 계획 
   영업현금 확대·투자 부담 완화에 따른 잉여 재원으로 차입금 감축 기조 지속. 구체적 조달 규모는 미제시

Q4-2. 유럽 IAA 정책 방향에 대응한 중장기 점유율 확보 전략은? 
  A. IAA는 아직 최종안 미확정이나 수정 권고안상 외국인 직접투자·현지화 요건 강화 예상 
   배터리뿐 아니라 주요 소재의 현지 생산 역량 중요성이 확대될 전망 
   유럽 OEM 의 공급망 다변화로 현지 생산 체계를 갖춘 비중국 업체 소싱 니즈 확대, 경쟁 환경은 우호적 판단 
   폴란드 현지 양산 및 소재·부품 공급망을 이미 구축해 현지 배터리 소싱 수요에 신속 대응 가능 
   고전압 Mid-Ni 에 대한 유럽 고객 관심 증가. 폴란드 감가상각비 감소도 가격 경쟁력 확보에 기여 
   고전압 Mid-Ni 외 LFP·CTP 등 다양한 제품·솔루션으로 신규 수주를 확보해 유럽 점유율 확대 추진

자료: LS증권 리서치센터

## Document 287: AI安全测试引发黑客事件，以色列公司承认系人为失误所致

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI安全测试引发黑客事件，以色列公司承认系人为失误所致.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

以⾊列⽹络安全公司 Irregular Security ⾸席执⾏官 Dan Lahav 公开承认，近期涉及
Anthropic 、 OpenAI 、 Meta 及⾕歌四⼤ AI 巨头的⽹络攻击事件，根源在于公司内部测试配
置失误 —— 安全隔离控制未正确设置，导致 AI 模型突破沙盒环境并⼊侵外部系统。 Lahav
强调，这属于流程管理失误。
摘要：
以⾊列⽹络安全公司 Irregular Security ⾸席执⾏官承认，近期涉及多家顶级 AI 模型开发商的⼀系
列⽹络攻击事件，根源在于该公司内部的⼈为失误。
11 ⽉ 3 ⽇周⼆，据彭博报道， Irregular Security ⾸席执⾏官 Dan Lahav 在伦敦彭博科技峰会上表
⽰，测试期间应有的安全隔离控制未被正确配置，导致 AI 模型突破封闭的沙盒测试环境，接⼊外
部互联⽹，并在部分案例中对外部⽹站及计算机系统实施了可能违法的⼊侵⾏为。
Lahav 此番公开表态，是迄今最直接的说明，但部分⽹络安全专家此前已指出，若安全护栏配置

得当，这些事件本可避免。
Anthropic 、 OpenAI 、 Meta 及⾕歌均已披露了相关事件。
这⼀系列事件在全球范围内引发对 AI 发展速度的⼴泛警惕，并加剧了外界对前沿模型是否已超出
⼈类有效管控的担忧。
配置失误是根本原因，⾮模型主动绕过安全机制
Dan Lahav 在演讲中明确区分了两种不同性质的失败：是测试配置本⾝出错，⽽⾮ AI 模型主动颠
覆安全机制。 Dan Lahav 说：
这⼀定性对于理解事件性质⾄关重要。若属于模型主动突破安全边界，将意味着 AI 系统已具备
对抗⼈类控制的能⼒，其影响将远⽐操作失误更为严峻。Lahav 的说法将事件归咎于流程管理层
⾯。
然⽽， Lahav ⾄今披露的技术细节仍然有限。今年 8 ⽉，他曾发表⼀篇逾 1.1 万字的博⽂，但该⽂
并未正⾯回应具体技术问题，⽽是着重探讨其所称的 "AI 安全⾛向的紧迫议题 " 。
四⼤ AI 巨头均涉其中，安全测试机制受审视
据彭博报道， Anthropic 、 OpenAI 、 Meta 及⾕歌在过去六个⽉内相继披露了⿊客事件，这些事件

均发⽣于 Irregular Security 主导的模型安全评估测试期间。
AI 模型评估旨在帮助开发商在产品公开发布前识别潜在⻛险并建⽴相应防护措施。
Irregular Security 采取的评估模式是深度嵌⼊前沿 AI 实验室内部，对模型进⾏测试。这种模式赋
予该公司⾼度的内部访问权限，使其站在了有关 AI 技术负责任部署这⼀核⼼议题的前沿位置。
由于⼀个失误导致误解，隔离防护措施未能按照特定测试的预期设置。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

## Document 320: Circle Q3营收、利润全面超预期，交易量同比增长49% _ 财报见闻

Source PDF: all report pdf/Circle Q3营收、利润全面超预期，交易量同比增长49% _ 财报见闻.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-04
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Circle 盘前涨逾 3% ，公司第三季度业绩全⾯超预期，总营收及储备收⼊达 8.65 亿美元，同
⽐增⻓ 17% ；调整后 EBITDA 同⽐⼤增 85% ⾄ 3.17 亿美元， ARC 代币预售贡献 1.82 亿美元
⼀次性收⼊。利率下⾏拖累核⼼储备收⼊，但 USDC 链上交易量同⽐增⻓ 49% 。
摘要：
稳定币发⾏商 Circle Internet Group 业绩全⾯超越市场预期， ARC 代币预售带来的⼀次性收⼊推
动调整后盈利能⼒⼤幅提升，股价盘前⾛⾼逾 3% 。
Circle 周三公布 2026 年第三季度财报，总营收及储备收⼊达 8.65 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 17% ，超出市
场预期的 7.767 亿美元；调整后 EBITDA 为 3.17 亿美元，同⽐⼤增 85% ，远超预期的 2.028 亿美
元；净利润 2.26 亿美元，亦显著⾼于市场预期的 9160 万美元。公司同步维持 2026 年全年业绩指
引不变。
本季度业绩亮点在很⼤程度上受益于 Arc 区块链主⽹上线所带来的 ARC 代币预售收⼊。这⼀⾮经

常性收⼊来源掩盖了储备收⼊下滑的压⼒ —— 后者因利率⾛低⽽同⽐下降 8% 。与此同时，
USDC 链上交易量同⽐⼤幅增⻓ 49%，显⽰出核⼼稳定币业务的强劲使⽤动能。
ARC 代币预售成为本季最⼤业绩变量
其他收⼊是本季最具弹性的收⼊来源，同⽐增⻓ 627% ⾄ 2.07 亿美元，其中 1.82 亿美元来⾃ ARC
代币预售。
Circle 于 9 ⽉ 16 ⽇正式启动 Arc 区块链公共主⽹，这是该公司⾃ USDC 发⾏以来规模最⼤的产品发
布，⾸⽇即有逾 100 家机构及⽣态建设者接⼊。与此同时， Circle 完成了 ARC 代币的创世铸造，
总供应量为 100 亿枚，成为⾸家为全新 Layer-1 区块链铸造⽹络代币的上市公司。 ARC 代币旨在
作为 Arc ⽹络安全、实⽤功能及治理的原⽣协调机制，⽹络计划于 2027 年完成向权益证明
（ Proof of Stake ）机制的过渡。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

Arc 的全球开发者社区⽬前拥有逾 75,000 名 Arc House 成员及 10,000 名 Architect ⼤使，后者在私
有主⽹期间共构建了逾 1,200 个项⽬。
储备收⼊承压，利率下⾏侵蚀核⼼利润
Circle 的储备收⼊为 6.58 亿美元，同⽐下降 8% ，主要原因是储备回报率下滑 61 个基点⾄ 3.5% ，
部分被 USDC 平均流通量同⽐增⻓ 8% ⾄ 733 亿美元所抵消。
从季度环⽐来看，储备收⼊已连续两个季度承压 ——2025 年第四季度储备收⼊为 7.33 亿美元，
此后逐季下滑，本季度降⾄ 6.58 亿美元。这⼀趋势反映出利率环境对 Circle 核⼼盈利模式的持续
影响。
分销成本的改善在⼀定程度上缓和了上述压⼒。本季度总分销、交易及其他成本为 4.12 亿美元，
同⽐下降 8% ，主要得益于分销付款减少，由此带动收⼊减去分销成本后的 RLDC 利润率升⾄
52% ，同⽐提升约 13 个百分点。
USDC 使⽤量加速扩张，机构合作密集落地
USDC 链上交易量本季度达到 13.9 万亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 49% ，链上资产规模较去年同期增⻓

36% 。持有超过 10 美元 USDC 的 " 有效钱包 " 数量达 730 万个，同⽐增⻓ 16% 。期末 USDC 稳定币
市场份额为 27% 。
本季度，围绕 USDC 的机构合作密集落地。 Visa 将其 Visa Direct 稳定币预充值业务从试点转⼊商
业部署，通过 Inyo Global ⽀持客⼾以 USDC 在逾 180 个国家和地区实现准实时跨境⽀付结算。
SAP ⽣态系公司 Tereina 宣布将 USDC 和 EURC 整合进其企业⽀付基础设施，涉及覆盖全球 84%
商业交易的 SAP ⽣态系统。三星钱包则已允许符合条件的美国 Galaxy ⽤⼾通过 USDC 向逾 60 个
国家的银⾏账⼾汇款。
此外，币安宣布对 Circle 进⾏ 1 亿美元股权投资，并扩⼤与 Circle 的战略合作伙伴关系，重点⾯向
新兴市场推⼴ USDC 。 Circle ⾸席执⾏官 Jeremy Allaire 表⽰， " 机构正在基于 USDC 进⾏构建和
扩展，这⼀趋势在本季度持续得到印证。 "

收购 Tazapay ，布局跨境⽀付新赛道
Circle ⽀付⽹络（ CPN ）本季度年化⽀付总额达 413 亿美元。为进⼀步拓展⽀付基础设施，
Circle 与新加坡跨境⽀付平台 Tazapay 签署最终收购协议，预计于 2027 年完成交割，届时仍需监
管部⻔批准。
Tazapay 年化⽀付规模逾 250 亿美元，拥有逾 60 家银⾏及⾦融科技合作伙伴，本地出⾦渠道覆盖
逾 100 个市场，且其约 60% 的交易量已涉及稳定币。 Circle 表⽰，此次收购将强化其在亚太、拉
美及新兴市场的⽀付发起与结算能⼒，⽽这些市场对 USDC 计价交易的需求正在增⻓。
裁员约 5% ，全年指引维持不变
本季度， Circle 完成约 5% 的裁员，以优化组织架构与经营模式的匹配度。与此次重组相关的费
⽤约为 915 万美元，主要包括通知期薪资、遣散费及员⼯福利⽀出。
尽管进⾏⼈员调整，公司仍维持 2026 年全年财务指引不变，包括：其他收⼊ 3.10 亿⾄ 3.30 亿美
元（含 ARC 代币预售已确认收⼊）； RLDC 利润率 41.7% ⾄ 43.7% ；调整后运营⽀出 5.70 亿⾄

5.85 亿美元。公司同时维持 USDC 流通量多年周期内 40% 复合年增⻓率的中⻓期指引。

• Average consumer spend across 
the Westpac-DataX Consumer 
Panel remained resilient, rising 
1.5% in Q3 and 4.6%yr. 
• While some of this reflected a price-
driven surge in fuel spend (+0.3%qtr) 
discretionary categories also posted 
robust gains (contributing 0.9ppts 
to the headline rise) with non-fuel 
essential categories contributing 
positively as well (+0.5ptps).
• Households continued to add to savings 
and buffers. Savings rose 1.3%qtr, led 
by younger households and mortgagors, 
with average savings buffers increasing 
to 22.2 months of mortgage repayments. 
• The detail shows financial pressures 
remain uneven but spending patterns 
appear to be converging across 
consumers with low, medium and high 
buffers. Much of this appears to relate 
to a reduction in fuel consumption.

Westpac–DataX Consumer Panel
The Westpac–DataX Consumer Panel is a large 
dataset that gives a timely and detailed picture 
of Australian consumer finances and behaviour. 
Developed by DataX, Westpac’s data analytics 
team, the dataset links transaction activity with 
balance sheet information to give a complete view 
of income, spending, saving and borrowing flows. 
All data is de-identified and aggregated to 
ensure privacy. The resulting sample of over 
one million customers gives an accurate 
representation of trends across the wider 
Australian consumer and is perfectly suited 
to quickly tracing responses to events like 
interest rate moves and energy price shocks.

Heatmap of monthly spending categories
The Westpac DataX Consumer Panel  shows average
consumer spending remained resilient in Q3, rising 1.5%
despite a further lift in fuel costs. Fuel contributed 0.3ppts
to quarterly growth, but discretionary spending did more

of the heavy lifting, contributing 0.9ppts as recreation
and travel-related spending recovered. Households also
continued to add to their financial buffers: savings up 1.3%
in the quarter and average buffers across households with
a mortgage increasing to 22.2 months of repayments.
Beneath the aggregate picture, lower-buffer households
remain more exposed to higher fuel costs and mortgage
repayments, but their discretionary spending share is
looking increasingly similar to other households.

Spending holds up despite higher fuel costs
Average household spending increased 1.5% in Q3, with much
of the strength again driven by transport-related spending.
Fuel contributed 0.3ppts to quarterly growth, while transport
excluding fuel contributed a further 0.4ppts. Excluding the
direct effects of the price-driven lift in fuel, average spend
was up 1.2%qtr. Importantly, stronger fuel spending did not
come at the expense of discretionary purchases. Discretionary

categories contributed 0.9ppts to quarterly spending growth,
compared with 0.5ppts from non-fuel essentials.
Solid rise in non-fuel spend and financial buffers
Discretionary spending dominates
Recreation and culture contributed 0.3ppts to growth, reflecting
stronger spending on sporting services, while restaurants and
hotels added a further 0.2ppts. Transport spending was also
boosted by a rebound in airline expenditure, which more than
unwound the weakness recorded in Q2 when refunds relating

to cancellations due to conflict in the Middle East weighed on
the category. Discretionary spending rose 0.9%qtr, following a
flat result and a 0.1% rise in the previous two quarters.

Savings growth by mortgage status
Households continue to rebuild savings
Savings increased 1.3% in Q3, with the strongest gains again 
recorded among younger households. Savings balances for 
18–34 year olds increased 2.0%qtr, compared with 0.7% 
for those aged 55 and over. Mortgagors also continued to 
accumulate savings more rapidly, recording a 2.8% increase 
compared with 0.7% among non-mortgagors. Savings growth 
among mortgagors has now outpaced non-mortgagors for 
three consecutive years.
Stronger savings have continued to lift mortgage buffers. 
Average buffers (savings divided by the sum of essential 
expenses and minimum mortgage repayments), increased to 
22.2 months of repayments in September. Looking within the 
unit-record sample, median mortgage buffers jumped from 
6.3 months in August to 6.8 months, its highest level since the 
series began in 2019.

Median mortgage buffers
Fuel spending a clear drag on consumers
Fuel spending as a share of total spending
Spending outcomes continued to vary across age groups. 
The squeeze remains most apparent among younger 
households. Fuel spending rose 5.1%qtr for 18–34 year olds, 
against a 1.5%qtr increase in non-fuel spending. By contrast, 
non-fuel spending increased 2.1%qtr among households aged 
55 and over. 
The gap between mortgagors and non-mortgagors was 
more modest. Spending rose 1.6%qtr among mortgagors 
and 1.9%qtr among those without a mortgage, suggesting 
mortgage status alone masks considerable variation in 
household financial positions – a divide that becomes clearer 
when households are grouped by the size of their mortgage 
buffers (see discussion further on in this note). Note that ‘non 
mortgagors’ covers a diverse range of consumers including 
those that are renting and those that own their homes without 
a mortgage.

### Page 4

WESTPAC ECONOMICS  
Median litres of fuel consumed
More importantly, their consumption appears to be less 
responsive to price movements. During periods of cheaper 
fuel, high-buffer households show a noticeably larger lift in 
litres purchased, consistent with stockpiling when prices are 
favourable. Low-buffer households show much less of this 
response, while their fuel volumes have also adjusted less 
during the recent increase in fuel costs.
The spending data show the consequence. Fuel expenditure 
has risen sharply across all three groups recently, but remains 
highest among low-buffer households, where it also absorbs 
a larger share of total spending. Food spending has also 
declined since 2023. This may partly reflect households 
economising within the category, including switching towards 
cheaper or home-brand products, although further work is 
needed to test this.
Taken together, the households with the smallest savings 
cushions face higher mortgage repayments, spend more 
on fuel and appear to have less scope to adjust their fuel 
consumption when prices change. 
Discretionary spend as a share of total
This suggests that in aggregate, household balance sheets 
are looking healthy. The picture is a little at odds with the very 
weak reads on our Westpac-Melbourne Index Consumer 
Sentiment Index which suggests finances are coming under 
much more pressure. The fact that both average spending and 
saving both rose suggests incomes over the quarter were not 
overly stretched and that more of the weakness in sentiment 
may relate to concerns about the outlook (something that may 
also be encouraging consumers to build buffers now, while they 
still can).
Pressures intense for low-buffer households
Aggregate buffer measures mask sizeable differences across 
mortgagors. We can get a sense of these variations by 
combining mortgagors into three broad groups according to 
buffer size, i.e. low, middle and high buffer groups. This shows 
that households with the smallest buffers are also more 
exposed to both interest rates and fuel costs.

The buffer differences are relatively stark, the median savings 
of the low-buffer group just $2k compared to $15k for the 
middle-buffer group and $95k for the high buffer group. 
Low buffer households face median minimum mortgage 
repayments of around $2,600 a month, compared with $2,400 
for the middle buffer group and $2,200 for the highest-buffer 
group. 
Low buffer households also spend more on fuel, at around 
$170 a month compared with $160 for the middle buffer 
group and $150 for the high buffer group.

We can use average pump prices to estimate what this 
expenditure implies for fuel volumes. Low-buffer households 
consume around 70 litres a month, middle-buffer households 
67 litres a month and the high-buffer group around 62 litres. 
This compares to an average of 81, 80 and 70 respectively 
through 2024-25. 
Median fuel spend by customer

### Page 5

Past performance is not a reliable indicator of future performance. The forecasts given above are predictive in character. Whilst every effort has been taken to ensure that the assumptions on which the forecasts are based are reasonable, 
the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
5
WESTPAC ECONOMICS  
Yet despite this greater exposure, the gap in broader spending 
behaviour has narrowed. High-buffer households continue 
to spend more on discretionary categories, averaging around 
$1,940 a month compared with $1,740 for low-buffer 
households. However, their discretionary share has been 
broadly stable since 2023, while the shares of low- and 
middle-buffer households have risen by close to a percentage 
point bringing them more in line with the high-buffer group.

Some of the convergence reflects a longer run down-trend in 
fuel spending that has been more pronounced amongst low 
and middle buffer households. This lowering in fuel spend lifts 
the share available for other categories.

Essential spending excluding fuel, meanwhile, has changed 
little across the three cohorts since 2023. The shift in 
spending patterns has largely occurred on the discretionary 
side.

The result is a somewhat mixed picture. Low-buffer 
households remain considerably more exposed to mortgage 
and fuel costs and have far smaller savings cushions. But their 
discretionary spending has nevertheless moved closer to that 
of better-buffered households, suggesting some recovery in 
spending flexibility despite these constraints. 
Conclusion
Aggregate household finances remain resilient: spending and 
savings both increased in Q3 and mortgage buffers continued 
to rebuild. Discretionary spending has also held up despite the 
sharp increase in fuel costs.

But aggregate resilience masks a much more uneven 
distribution of pressure. Low-buffer households 
simultaneously face higher mortgage repayments, greater fuel 
exposure and much smaller savings cushions. While these 
households have so far maintained discretionary spending, 
their capacity to absorb further pressure is clearly more 
limited.
About the Westpac-DataX Consumer Panel
Developed by DataX, Westpac’s data analytics service for 
institutional and business banking customers, the dataset links 
transaction activity with balance sheet information to give 
a complete view of income, spending, saving and borrowing 
flows. 
The resulting sample of over one million customers provides 
an accurate representation of trends across the wider 
Australian consumer. Monthly observations are available back 
to January 2019 with timely updates provided a few weeks 
after the end of each month. As such, it is perfectly suited to 
quickly tracing the impact and responses to events like interest 
rate moves and energy price shocks.

## Document 331: Could Midterms Give Wall Street A Cold Shower_

Source PDF: all report pdf/Could Midterms Give Wall Street A Cold Shower_.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-11-03
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: Could Midterms Give Wall Street A Cold Shower?
Date:    1 hour ago
Could Midterms Give Wall Street A Cold Shower?

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
Could Midterms Give Wall Street
A Cold Shower?
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 2 Nov 2026
View in browser

I. When Gridlock Isn't Good for Stocks
The US midterm elections will be over tomorrow night. According to
betting markets, Democrats have roughly a 95% probability of winning the
House and a 76% probability of taking the Senate (charts).
We suspect that stock investors have yet to fully discount the possibility of
a Democratic sweep of Congress. Some may remain skeptical that polls
confirming strong support for Democrats are accurate. They might reckon

### Page 2

that Democrats are more likely than Republicans to talk to pollsters over
the phone.
In any event, Wall Street traditionally welcomes political gridlock because
it reduces the likelihood that Congress will pass extremist legislation. But
this time could be different for two reasons.
First, deepening partisanship could turn gridlock into damaging political
paralysis at a time when the Bond Vigilantes are already uneasy about
Washington's unsustainable fiscal trajectory. A Democratic sweep could

complicate negotiations over government spending and the debt ceiling.
Prolonged shutdowns and fiscal standoffs could push Treasury yields
higher, weighing on equity valuations.

### Page 3

Second, gridlock's traditional ability to constrain policymaking may not
hold this time. President Donald Trump has relied heavily on executive
authority, with some of his most market-disruptive policies, including tariffs
and military action against Iran, pursued largely without congressional
approval. A Democratic-controlled Congress may therefore do little to
restrain him. Moreover, with the midterms behind him, Trump may feel
even less constrained politically and more willing to pursue controversial

policies through executive action. Hence, a blue wave could deliver a cold
shower for stocks, though we would view a significant selloff as a buying
opportunity given the remarkable strength of the economy and corporate
earnings.
One encouraging development ahead of the elections was today's reports
that the US and Iran are working on a peaceful diplomatic solution to their
war. Iranian President Masoud Pezeshkian said he has granted his
government greater authority to pursue peace talks, while President

Trump and Vice President Vance also signaled progress. Hopes for a deal
sent the price of Brent crude oil down 7.6% to $104.77 per barrel (chart).
However, we have been here before with apparent diplomatic
breakthroughs. We still see a high risk of military re-escalation. The bid-
ask spread remains wide, with Tehran demanding sanctions relief, the
release of frozen assets, and an end to the US naval blockade of Iranian
ports, while Washington insists on nuclear concessions. Meanwhile, Iran's

leverage over the Strait of Hormuz has been weakening. According to
shipping data, for roughly half of September, Gulf oil exporters shipped
more crude than they were shipping before the war.

### Page 4

II. US Economy Continues To Roar
The US economy remains strong despite the volatility of the domestic
political and geopolitical scenes. Consider the following:
(1) Construction spending. Construction spending fell 1.9% m/m in
September to $2.19 trillion (saar), reversing most of August's upwardly
revised 2.0% increase. Nevertheless, spending remains just below last
year's record high (chart).
September's decline was driven by a 5.5% m/m drop in private residential

construction, attributable to a 13.0% plunge in home improvements
(chart). Elevated materials costs and higher mortgage rates continue to
weigh on the housing sector. Meanwhile, private nonresidential
construction rose 1.1% m/m, supported by continued investment in data
centers and power infrastructure.

### Page 5

The AI investment boom remains a powerful tailwind. Data center
construction spending surged 8.6% m/m and 90% y/y in September,
reaching a record $96.5 billion annual rate (chart).
(2) Manufacturing. The ISM M-PMI rose from 54.5 in September to 55.5
in October, marking its tenth consecutive month of expansion, the longest
streak since 2022. Production jumped to the highest reading since
November 2021, while new orders edged up to 55.5. Employment also

remained in expansionary territory (chart).

### Page 6

Inflationary pressures remain troublesome. The prices-paid index eased
from 77.9 to 75.2 in October but remained above 70 for the ninth
consecutive month. Regional Fed surveys also point to persistent price
pressures. Higher energy costs, tariffs, and elevated metals prices
continue to boost input costs (chart).
(3) Bank lending. The Fed's latest Senior Loan Officer Opinion Survey
(SLOOS) painted a mixed picture of credit demand. Banks reported
weaker demand for commercial and industrial (C&I) loans, mortgages,

and auto loans in Q4, while credit-card demand strengthened (chart).

### Page 7

Lending standards remained broadly unchanged for C&I loans but
tightened somewhat for commercial real estate loans (chart).
Encouragingly, actual bank lending tells a happier story. C&I loans surged
11.3% y/y through October 21, while total loans and leases increased
7.9% y/y (chart).

### Page 8

Credit growth is broad across bank sizes, with lending up 8.1% y/y at
large banks and 5.4% at small banks (chart).
Meanwhile, banks' allowances for loan and lease losses have remained
relatively stable at around $202 billion, despite the acceleration in lending
(chart).

### Page 9

clause.

## Document 371: Hypers大涨、Neocloud暴跌：民主党横扫国会，美股科技板块上演大轮动

Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-05
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

CoreWeave 跌 10% ， Hut 8 跌超 20% ， AI 基建监管趋严，⾼度依赖融资的 AI 数据中⼼运营
商⼤跌。亚⻢逊和微软各涨 6% ，市场认为若数据中⼼新建放缓，现有资产的稀缺价值上
升， AI 资本开⽀被抑制，利好超⼤规模云⼚商。此外， SpaceX 与特斯拉跌超 3% ，⻢斯克
相关资产因政治⻛险上升⽽⼤跌。特朗普提出的 1.5 万亿美元国防预算或受阻，国防板块普
跌。清洁能源与医疗服务受益于⺠主党政策⽽上涨。
摘要：
⺠主党横扫国会两院，触发美股剧烈板块轮动。数据中⼼运营商、国防承包商及与特朗普相关
资产遭到抛售，清洁能源、医疗保险等板块则⼤幅反弹，市场正在重新定价⼀个监管更趋严
格、政策优先项发⽣位移的新格局。
此次⺠主党胜选范围超出市场预期，不仅突破共和党传统票仓，更赢得德克萨斯州和佐治亚州
两⼤ AI 数据中⼼重镇。
德克萨斯州选出逾 30 年来⾸位⺠主党籍州⻓ Gina Hinojosa ，其竞选纲领明确主张对数据中⼼建
设实施临时暂停令，令相关股票雪上加霜。与此同时，原本被市场视为在共和党国会畅⾏⽆阻

的 1.5 万亿美元国防预算扩张预期亦随之落空。⺠主党掌控国会将使过去两年共和党主导下的监
管宽松路径出现逆转， AI 基础设施投资的政策红利⾯临重新评估，⽽清洁能源、医疗保障等此前
受压板块则将获得更强的⽴法⽀撑。
然⽽，科技板块内部出现明显分裂。在 AI 数据中⼼运营商（ Neocloud ）⼤跌之际，云⼤⼚
（ Hyperscalers ，超⼤规模云服务商）逆势暴涨，微软涨、亚⻢逊均涨超 6% 。资⾦从依赖新建
算⼒的标的出逃，同时涌⼊⼿握现成算⼒的超⼤规模云⼚商。市场认为若数据中⼼新建放缓，
现有资产的稀缺价值上升， AI 资本开⽀被抑制，利好超⼤规模云⼚商。
⾼盛交易台的约翰 · 弗勒德（ John Flood ）点评称：
德州易主：数据中⼼运营商遭⾎洗
数据中⼼运营商是这场选举最直接的受害者。
Hut 8 跌近 23% ， Cipher Digital 重挫 21% ， Core Scientific 和 IREN 跌幅均超 10% ， Applied
Digital 下跌 6.7% ⾄ 8.8% ， CoreWeave 下跌约 9.5% 。作为数据中⼼建设最⼤受益者，英伟达股

价当⽇⼤跌 3.17% 。
⽬前我们交易台最突出的主题是买⼊超⼤规模云⼚商（ Hyperscalers ），同时卖出半导体
（ Semis ）。为什么要买⼊超⼤规模云⼚商？如果由于⽆法建设更多产能导致算⼒变得更加稀
缺，你就可以以更⾼的价格出售它。如果你⽆法建设更多产能，你的资本⽀出就会减少。如果不
把现⾦⽤于产能建设，你就极有可能利⽤这些闲置资⾦重新开始回购股票。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

打击来⾃⼀个具体的地名：德克萨斯州。
根据研究机构 SemiAnalysis 数据，德州占美国 2030 年预期上线算⼒约四分之⼀，多家机构预估
⼤量德州规划的数据中⼼产能⾯临搁置或延期⻛险。⾏业测算显⽰， 2027 年约两成超⼤规模云
⼚商资本开⽀对应的前期建设⼯作存在不确定性， 2028 年新增产能受冲击⽐例更⾼。
⺠主党候选⼈ Gina Hinojosa 赢得德州州⻓选举，成为逾 30 年来该州⾸位⺠主党籍州⻓，其竞选
纲领明确主张对数据中⼼建设实施临时暂停令。瑞银全球宏观股票策略及美国股票策略研究主
管 Keith Parker 在给客⼾的报告中写道，出⼝⺠调显⽰，选⺠强烈反对在其社区新建数据中⼼。
Clear Street 颠覆性技术股权研究董事总经理 Brian Dobson 指出：
为数据中⼼建设提供电⼒与设备⽀持的企业同样承压。 GE Vernova 下跌 11.5% ，在德州数据中
⼼项⽬敞⼝最⼤的两家电⼒公司 Constellation Energy 和 Vistra 分别下跌 9.2% 和 15.4% 。

Siebert Financial ⾸席投资官 Mark Malek 表⽰：
分析认为，不会出现数据中⼼全⾯关停，但会迎来更多审批繁⽂缛节，⼤量规划项⽬延后，进
⼊建设慢、周期拉⻓的状态。多头甚⾄提出企业会把资本开⽀前置到 2027 年抢电⼒与许可，但
市场多数参与者认为半导体板块尚未充分定价这⼀轮周期下⾏压⼒。
这股 ' 蓝⾊浪潮 ' 将对股市产⽣影响，短期内最⼤的影响可能来⾃州层⾯对数据中⼼监管的加强。
" 德克萨斯州政治格局的转变可能带来额外的数据中⼼监管措施，这将有利于拥有良好企业公⺠
记录的⽼牌机构。 "
"AI 基础设施建设不会消失，只会变得更贵。税收优惠减少、电⼒和⽔资源成本责任增加，将压
缩回报空间，令利润率对投资者⽽⾔愈发关键。最脆弱的 AI 企业，是那些⾼度依赖融资的公司，
⽽⾮规模最⼤的资本⽀出⽅。 "

### Page 3

Evercore ISI 分析师团队在研究报告中指出：“ 若数据中⼼开发环境趋于收紧，将对 AI 基础设施
相关股票带来更多负⾯情绪。 ”
也有声⾳认为抛售过度。 LO:TECH 研究主管 Adam McCarthy 指出，特朗普政府⼀贯强⼒⽀持 AI
发展，针对数据中⼼概念股的⼤幅下跌看似反应过度，已建成并完成转型改造的矿机基础设
施，在监管收紧的环境下反⽽更具价值。
建得越少反⽽越值钱：云⼤⼚单⽇暴增万亿市值
截⾄周三收盘，微软涨 6.7% ，亚⻢逊涨 6.1% ，⾕歌⺟公司 Alphabet 涨 3.2% ， Meta 涨 1.4% ，苹
果涨 5.3% 。五⼤科技巨头单⽇市值合计增加近 1 万亿美元。
市场逻辑在于：若数据中⼼新建步伐放缓，现有资产的稀缺价值将随之上升，同时有望抑制⼤
型科技公司不断膨胀的 AI 资本⽀出。投资者此前持续因举债加码 AI 投资、回报前景不明确⽽惩罚
这些股票。
B. Riley Wealth ⾸席市场策略师 Art Hogan 表⽰：
Dakota Wealth Management ⾼级投资组合经理 Robert Pavlik 说得更直⽩：

这句话触及了问题的核⼼。近期，亚⻢逊、微软、 Alphabet 和 Meta 的股价持续承压，原因是亚
⻢逊、 Alphabet 和 Meta 的⾃由现⾦流已转为负值 —— 资本开⽀超过了经营产⽣的现⾦，这些公
司不得不依靠发债和股权融资来⽀撑投⼊。Pavlik 进⼀步指出：
超⼤规模云⼚商已拥有现成的数据中⼼，数据中⼼不再被⼤量新建，意味着这些资产更具价值。
建设节奏放缓，资本⽀出的步伐也会随之趋缓，这对超⼤规模云⼚商⽽⾔是确定性的正⾯影响。
市场表现得就好像这预⽰着 AI 基建热潮的终结。如果这意味着超⼤规模云服务商不再砸下天⽂数
字般的资⾦去扩建算⼒，那么它们的⾃由现⾦流有望重新转正。

### Page 4

除了存量资产增值，市场还预期⼤⼚可以把算⼒建设向⻢来西亚等海外地区转移，对冲美国本
⼟审批阻⼒，进⼀步强化其相对于新兴云⼚商的竞争壁垒。
⻢斯克相关资产：政治⻛险溢价显现
特斯拉下跌 3.3% ， SpaceX 股价下跌 3.5% ⾄ 8 ⽉中旬以来低位。⻢斯克此前投⼊近 5 亿美元⽀持
共和党候选⼈，其中包括帮助共和党维持对德克萨斯州控制权的相关⾏动，如今其政治对⼿重
夺国会控制权。
XTB 研究总监 Kathleen Brooks 表⽰，此次选举结果令外界对 SpaceX 与特斯拉潜在合并计划产⽣
质疑。
特朗普概念加密资产全线下挫
据 CoinGecko 数据，特朗普联合创办的 World Liberty Financial 旗下 WLFI 代币过去 24 ⼩时跌幅约
7% ，以其名字命名的 Meme 币跌幅约 17% 。
World Liberty Financial 发⾔⼈ David Wachsman 则表⽰，该公司 " ⾃成⽴以来，始终遵守所有适
⽤的美国法律法规 " ，并称 " 国会若⽆端针对⼀家致⼒于扩⼤美元普及的合规公司，令⼈担忧 " 。

超⼤规模云服务商此前陷⼊两难：⼀⽅⾯因⼤⼿笔开⽀⽽受到惩罚，另⼀⽅⾯若缩减开⽀同样会
招来质疑。从某种意义上说，这场选举是送给超⼤规模云服务商的⼀份礼物 —— 给它们的⽀出踩
下刹⻋的，不是企业⾃⼰，⽽是政府。
" 国会虽⽆法阻⽌合并，但可通过加强审查的⽅式拖延进程。
⻢斯克为共和党投⼊了数亿美元竞选资⾦，如今其政治对⼿重夺国会控制权，与⻢斯克相关的股
票可能持续承压。 "

### Page 5

加密资产研究通讯《 Crypto is Macro Now 》作者 Noelle Acheson 指出，此轮跌势 " 既反映了相关
资产光环的消退，也折射出市场对潜在调查或司法追诉的预期 " 。
同时，特朗普媒体与科技集团股价⼀度重挫 17% ，创历史新低；追踪该股的杠杆 ETF 产品 DJTU
跌幅达 32% 。
国防承包商：预算扩张预期受挫
国防板块普跌，诺思罗普 · 格鲁曼（ Northrop Grumman ）下跌 3.7% ， RTX 、洛克希德 · ⻢丁
（ Lockheed Martin ）和 L3Harris 跌幅均在 3% ⾄ 6% 之间。
此前市场预期特朗普提出的 1.5 万亿美元国防预算在共和党控制的国会将畅⾏⽆阻，但⺠主党接
掌国会令这⼀路径⽣变。
eToro 全球市场策略师 Lale Akoner 表⽰：
清洁能源：政策预期驱动反弹
清洁能源板块全线⾛⾼。
First Solar 涨 8.4% ，居家太阳能安装商 Sunrun 涨 6.9% ，逆变器制造商 Enphase Energy 涨

8.8% ，清洁能源开发商 NextEra Energy 涨 4.6% 。欧洲市场同步反应，丹⻨ Vestas 涨 2.9% ，
Orsted 涨 4.6% ，葡萄⽛ EDP Renewables 涨 2.8% 。
投资者押注⺠主党掌控国会将为清洁能源提供更强有⼒的联邦政策⽀撑，同时增加对推翻排放
法规相关努⼒的阻⼒。⺠主党还可能借助《国会审查法》叫停放宽燃油⻋销售限制的相关法
规。
Invesco EMEA 全球市场策略师 Paul Jackson 表⽰， " 油⽓价格上涨越多，推⼴清洁能源的经济
动⼒就越强。 "
医疗上涨、私营监狱下跌：政策⽅向决定股价
医疗板块受益于政策预期转向。医院运营商 HCA Healthcare 和 Universal Health Services 分别上
涨 8.5% 和 6.8% ，保险商 Centene 和 Molina Healthcare 分别上涨 7.3% 和 6.3% 。
摩根⼠丹利分析师在研报中指出：
" 艰难的债务上限谈判可能推迟⽀出增加，更依赖新政府合同的企业将⽐合同基础稳固的企业⾯

临更⼤的⻛险。真正的⻛险在于，⽀出增速低于预期，从⽽引发价格修正。 "
" 此次选举结果将为国会推进⺠主党医疗政策优先项提供更⼤空间，包括恢复《平价医疗法案》
强化补贴，或逆转共和党医疗补助削减措施中的就业要求规定。
尽管我们仍认为在下次总统⼤选周期前出现实质性医疗政策变化的可能性较低，但选举结果可能
改变政策叙事⾛向。 "

## Document 392: Meta首次叩门欧元债市，530亿欧元认购需求创历史纪录

Source PDF: all report pdf/Meta首次叩门欧元债市，530亿欧元认购需求创历史纪录.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-04
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Meta ⾸次发⾏欧元债券获 530 亿欧元创纪录认购需求，计划通过六档债券筹集⾄多 90 亿欧
元，期限最⻓达 38 年。此前， Meta 刚完成 150 亿美元美元债发⾏。科技巨头密集融资之
际， AI 基础设施投资规模及资本开⽀回报正受到市场更严格审视。
摘要：
Meta Platforms ⾸次进军欧元债券市场，吸引投资者创纪录的认购需求。
据彭博周三报道，知情⼈⼠透露，Meta ⾸次发⾏欧元债券获得 530 亿欧元（约合 597 亿美元）的
投资者认购需求，创下历史纪录。 Meta 此次计划通过六档欧元债券发⾏筹集⾄多 90 亿欧元，预
计于周三晚些时候定价。
此次发⾏是 Meta ⾸次在美国本⼟市场之外发⾏债券。就在此前⼀天， Meta 刚刚完成 150 亿美元
的美元债发⾏。连续登陆美元和欧元债市，凸显出这家科技巨头正积极拓展融资渠道，为⼈⼯
智能（ AI ）基础设施投资提供资⾦⽀持。
六档债券覆盖近 40 年期限
据知情⼈⼠透露， Meta 此次欧元债券发⾏分为六档，期限分别为 4 年、 6 年、 8 年、 12 年、 18 年

及 38 年以上。
其中，最短期限债券的初步定价指引较中期互换利率溢价约 140 个基点，最⻓期限债券的利差指
引约为 370 个基点。花旗集团和摩根⼠丹利担任此次发⾏的主承销商。
投资者对 Meta 美元债发⾏的需求同样强劲。周⼆，该公司 150 亿美元美元债交易获得最⾼ 580 亿
美元认购需求。
科技巨头密集融资， AI 投资规模引发关注
Meta 此次发⾏欧元债券，正值全球⼤型科技公司密集进⼊债券市场融资之际。
⾃上周末以来， Meta 、微软、甲⻣⽂和 Alphabet 四家科技巨头合计融资近 570 亿美元。此前，科
技企业曾连续三个⽉暂停在美国投资级债券市场发⾏债券，微软上周以 250 亿美元债券发⾏打破
这⼀空窗期，随后甲⻣⽂⼜完成 100 亿美元融资。
这⼀轮融资潮的背后，是科技巨头为⼈⼯智能建设投⼊的巨额资⾦需求。数据中⼼、芯⽚及其
他基础设施建设正推动科技企业加速融资。
不过，随着 AI 基础设施投资规模不断扩⼤，市场对相关⽀出的审视也⽇益加剧。投资者开始更加
关注科技巨头在数据中⼼和 AI 基础设施上的投⼊。

与此同时，美国中期选举也为科技⾏业带来新的政治不确定性。⺠主党刚刚赢得国会控制权，
市场开始关注其对⼤型科技公司的态度是否会发⽣变化。
彭博指出，数据中⼼建设引发的选⺠不满已成为此次选举中的重要议题。在俄亥俄州等地，围
绕数据中⼼建设的争议甚⾄影响了选举结果。

## Document 434: The nowcasts, updated scenario and forecasts of the Economic Research - 2 November 2026 (2026-11-02)

Source PDF: all report pdf/The nowcasts, updated scenario and forecasts of the Economic Research -  2 November 2026 (2026-11-02).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

economic
-
research.bnpparibas.com

Check out the economic research nowcasts on November 9. 
In the meantime, you can read our analysis of the third
-
quarter growth figures 
here

UNITED STATES 
The US economy is expected to grow above its potential in 2026, with an average annual growth rate of 2. 2%, close to the 2025 rate (2.1%) . 
This apparent resilience to the uncertainty, tariff and energy shocks, together with above-trend productivity growth, masks K-shaped growth, 
driven by investment linked to AI-optimism and consumption by the wealthiest amid a wealth effect (historically high stock market valuations). 
Inflation overshooting is set to continue (3.5% in 2026) at least through 2028, largely because of the rise in oil prices and tariffs – although the 
impact of these latter appears to be less significant than expected. The labour market is showing clear signs of improvement that should bring 
the unemployment rate down toward 4.0%. Given this shift in price and employment risks amid dynamic growth, the FOMC proceeded with a 
first rate hike at September’s meeting and we expect two further 25bp increases in December 2026 and January 2027, bringing the Fed Funds 
target range at 4.25% - 4.50%.

CHINA 
Economic growth rebounded to 4.5% y/y in Q3 2026 (1.2% q/q) after 4.3% y/y in Q2 and 5.0% in Q1. It is projected to reach 4.6% in 2026 as a 
whole, down from 5% in 2025. Growth remains characterised by a K-shaped trajectory, and the gap between the strong performance of exports 
and the fragility of sectors that rely on domestic demand has widened this year. Retail sales barely increased in the first nine months of 2026, 
the crisis in the property sector has continued and household confidence remains low. Fiscal policy measures aimed at supporting investment 
and private consumption will increase over the last months of the year . CPI inflation has rebounded slightly since last March, mostly due to 
the rise in global energy prices, as well as due to the increase in electronic good prices and anti-involution measures implemented by the 
authorities. Deflationary pressures persist, however, given weakness in domestic demand.

The scenario, forecasts and nowcasts of the Economic Research 
–

ber 2026

### Page 2

EUROZONE 
Eurozone growth is expected to slow to 1.1% in 2026 (from 1.3% in 2025), before strengthening to 1.6% in 2027, supported by higher defence 
and infrastructure spending as well as the global AI capex cycle. The economy has proven resilient to the energy shock, notab ly thanks to the 
increasingly tangible impact of German defence spending on the real economy . Elevated energy prices in the autumn (especially for natural 
gas), combined with resilient growth, are set to accelerate inflation to a peak around the turn of the year. Annual average i nflation is thus 
expected to reach 3.1% in 2026 (from 2.1% in 2025), and 3.2% in 2027. Given persistent inflation and resilient growth, we anticipate another 
25bp hike from the ECB in December 2026, bringing the deposit rate to 2.75%, followed by an extended pause. That said, strong er than 
expected second-round effects could lead to more tightening than our current baseline.

FRANCE 
GDP contracted by 0.1% q/q  in Q3 2026 (following stagnation in Q2), weighed down by a decline in aero nautics production (delay in the 
delivery of inputs) but supported by service sector output and purchases of electric vehicles. Overall, growth is expected to slow to 0.3% in 
2026 (after 0.9% in 2025) before  rebounding to 0.8% in 2027. In parallel, inflation should reach 2.5% in 2026 (compared with 0.9% in 2025), 
before edging down slightly in 2027 (2.4%).

UNITED KINGDOM 
UK GDP growth would improve slightly to 1.3% in 2026 and remain at this level in 2027  (1.2% in 2025). The economy has so far weathered 
both the energy price shock and domestic political volatility. That said, growth is expected to decelerate in the second half  of the year, while 
inflation is set to remain stubbornly above target (averag ing 3.2% in 2026 and 3.5% in 2027), driven by higher energy prices and mounting 
second-round effects. In response, we expect the BoE to deliver a single insurance hike in November 2026, bringing the Bank Rate to 4.00%. 
The BoE is then expected to resume easing in H2 2027, with two cuts taking the Bank Rate to 3.50% by end-year.

JAPAN 
Following 1.2% in 2025, Japan's GDP growth is expected to decelerate to 1.0% in 2026, before rebounding to 1.2% in 2027. Higher inflation 
and production costs associated with the energy shock are expected to weigh on the economy’s overall performance. On the other hand, the 
latter is supported by fiscal policy  and AI-related investment. Inflation has generally overshot the 2% y/y target since 2022. Accordingly, the 
Bank of Japan initiated a cautious process of “adjustment in the degree of monetary accommodation” in 2024, lifting the policy rate to 1.25% 
so far (previously negative) – the highest since 1995. We expect two more hikes by the end of Q1 2027, followed by a return to a more cautious 
pace until the terminal rate of 2.5% is reached in H2 2028. Japan is facing long-term rates pressure, illustrated by historically high 10- and 30-
year yields, probably fueled by the level of public debt and the pace of monetary adjustment.

EXCHANGE RATES 
We expect Fed hikes to temporarily offset structural headwinds and support the dollar. EUR/USD would target 1.14 by end -2026, before the 
dollar's structural downtrend resumes toward 1.20 by end-2027, although political and fiscal risk may weigh on the euro next year. The USD/JPY 
appreciation would continue but at a more moderate pace, with the BoJ still behind the curve despite its hikes. By contrast, GBP/USD would 
see relative support underpinned by a modest fiscal risk premium under the new Labour government, and resilient growth.
